If the sign of square is on the region of Sun, then the person leads a very high standard of life regarding his honour, respect, post and fame. The fame of his works spreads all over.

If there is a square on the mount of Mercury, he escapes imprisonment.

A square mark on the mount of Moon increases the power of imagination of a person. Such a person is sober and kind. He keeps patience even in opposing circumstances.

If there is a mark of square on the Dragon's Tail, then such a person should expect early rise of fortune. He spends a jolly good time in his youth.

If there is a square mark on the mount of Venus, then the person takes caution in love affairs and thus escapes scandals.

If there is a square on the mount of Mars, one is able to contain his anger. He gets angry rarely in his life.

A sign of square on the line of travelling denotes that the incumbent will go on journey several times and that he will earn wealth from his travels.

A sign of square on the line of Moon assists a person in all sorts of progress.

If there is a sign of square on the marriage line then the person has an educated, beautiful and amiable wife. He also gets wealth from his in-laws.

If there is a sign of square on the health line then the personality of the person becomes fascinating. His health remains sound throughout his life.

If there is a square mark on the fate line then the fate of the person rises at an early age.

A mark of square on the line of Sun indicates honour, respect, high post and fame for the incumbent throughout his life.

If there is a sign of square on the heart line then his family life is full of happiness. Such a person is kind and generous by heart.

A sign of square on the head line indicates that the person will always have balance of mind and he remains active throughout his life.

If there is a square mark on the life line then the person enjoys longevity.

If there is a square mark on the Dragon's Head, then the incumbent spends enough time in the company of sages in forests.

In nutshell, the square mark in the palm at any place is considered auspicious.

7. Nets : The horizontal lines on the vertical lines form a sort of net. It is available anywhere in the palm. The results of such nets are as follows:

Nets

If there is a net on the region of Jupiter, then such a person is cruel, tyrant, selfish and proud.

If there is a net on the mount of Saturn, then the person is lazy. He suffers ill-fame due to his miserly habits.

If the net is on the mount of Sun, then the incumbent has to face ill-fame repeatedly.

If the net is on the mount of Mercury, then the person repents over his own doings and faces difficulties.

If there is a net on the region of Harshal, then the person surely commits a murder and has to suffer imprisonment.

If there is a net on the mount of Moon, then the person is fickle-minded and remains dissatisfied.

If there is a net on the Dragon's Tail, then the person remains perturbed throughout his life due to illnesses.

If there is net mark on the mount of Venus, then that person indulges in excessive dalliance and is licentious. He has no place whatsoever in the society.

If there is a net on the region of Mars, then the person remains impatient mentally throughout his life.

A net mark on the region of Dragon's Head indicates unavoidable unfortunate life for the incumbent.

If there is a net mark on the bracelets, then the incumbent faces a great downfall.

Nowhere in the palm is the sign of net considered favourable.

8. Stars : If we see minutely in the palm, we find stars at many places. Their results differ with their different places.

Stars

If there is a mark of star on the mount of Jupiter, then that person certainly achieves full success in his life. He does not lack wealth, respect, honour and high post in his life. He always ascends to progress and achieves a respectable post and a great success in the society.

If there is a sign of star on the mount of Saturn, then such a person gets rapid rise in fortune. He moves ahead towards his aim and gets fame and respect in his life.

If there is a star mark on the mount of Sun, then such a person gets full benefit of wealth in his life. Materially, he does not lack anything in his life. He also remains healthy— physically and mentally.

If there is a star mark on the mount of Mercury, then such a person becomes a successful businessman and a top planner. Such a person can also be a successful poet and writer.

If there is a mark of star on the Dragon's Tail, then that person's childhood is spent very happily and he does not need any material thing in his life.

If there is a star on the mount of Venus, then that person indulges in too much sexual activities. He has illicit relationship with other women as well. His wife remains very healthy and beautiful.

A star mark on the mount of Mars indicates that the person is courageous and patient. He gets national honour for his valour in war.

If there is a mark of star on the Dragon's Head, then his fortune always favours him. He gets full fame and honour in his life.

A star mark on the line of travelling shows that the person will die away from his house at a place of pilgrimage.

If there is a mark of star on the line of Moon, then that person suffers from stomach ailments and remains sick one way or the other.

If there is a mark of star on the line of Mars, then that person gets killed.

If there is a mark of star on the line of marriage, then that person faces many obstructions in his marriage and his family life cannot be termed as a happy one.

If there is a mark of star on the health line, then that person remains weak in health throughout his life and dies in miserable circumstances.

If there is a star on the Sun line, then the person gets extraordinary success in business and gets many fortunate moments to acquire wealth in his life.

If there is a star on the heart line, then he suffers from diseases related to heart.

If there is a sign of star on the head line, then the person suffers from diseases of the nerves for the whole of his life.

If a person has a mark of star on the life line, he dies suddenly in his youth.

If a person has a sign of star on the thumb, he is laborious, patient and successful.

A sign of star on the index finger is always quite auspicious.

Marks of stars on the finger of Saturn and other fingers make their respective stars stronger.

Hence, it is necessary to study a mark of star on the hand with utmost caution. These marks assist in the making of a personality and are also important in forecasting.

Determining the Time

In the previous chapters, I have dealt with certain facts about lines of the palm along with it, I have also stated about the assisting lines and signs or marks found on a hand. But with this, a natural question arises that, ‘when will a certain event happen in the life of a man?' To know and understand it is also very necessary.

In the life of a person, there are many perplexing questions— When will the fortune smile? How will it take place? Will it take place in this country or in a foreign country? When will one go abroad? When will one get a job? When will there be stability in the business? How much will one gain in business and when? What things or what works will give profit in the business? When will there be increase in income? When will one get promotion in the service? How much happiness will one get from one's children? When will one get married? and so on. These are the problems which crowd up in the mind of a person. For all these, it is therefore, very necessary to understand the process of determining time so that the foretelling may be clear and accurate.

In the previous pages, I have provided information regarding the heart line, the fate line, the health line, the head line and the life line. The study of life line is the most necessary.

As already stated earlier that the line which originates between the thumb and the index finger, moves circling the mount of Venus and reaches the bracelets is called the life line.

Firstly, for practice, one has to measure the life line with a thread from its starting point to the first line of the wrist. Considering the total length of the thread as a hundred years, it is to be divided into ten equal parts. Now one part will be equivalent to a period of ten years. This also is to be further divided into ten equal parts, Now each smaller part will be equivalent to a period of one year. After some practice, one will be able to assess correctly by just seeing the hand that how many years are represented by the life line of a person. If the life line ends abruptly then at that particular period one should assume that it is the end of one's life. It will definitely tell one's age. Wherever one finds a cross on the life line or it gets fade, then at that age, one suffers from a serious disease or may suffer from death-like misery. This should be understood.

All the events are indicated only by the life line in the hand. From a point on other lines, if one draws a straight line towards the life line, then the point where it touches the life line indicates the year in which a certain event will take place. To make it concrete, if there is a cut in the fate line at a certain point, then a straight line drawn from the cut towards the life line touches the life line at the age of 42. Thus we conclude that the individual will have a hurdle in his fortune at the age of 42 and he may have to face a big difficulty in his fate at that age.

In the same way, results of other lines can be calculated and the time of the occurrence of the events may also be known.

One should try this gradually. After such practice, just a view of the palm will tell one the related incident and its time.

A successful fortune-teller and an expert palmist will be recognized only when he is able to tell the correct time of an event and for that purpose, I have clarified the above point.

Procedure of Taking Hand-prints

M centre receives hundreds of letters from people desirous of knowing their future through the study of hand-prints. The services of this centre are available to the people of this country and also to those living in foreign countries and it is a matter of great happiness that people have certainly availed the opportunity so offered to them by my centre. Though studying hands personally is always most preferable to studying the hand-prints because of the obvious reason that one can see the protrusion of the mounts only when shown personally and also that even the smallest lines can also be studied when shown the hand personally. But this facility is not available for all. Those who are far away and those who live in foreign countries, for them hand-print is the only medium through which they can know their future.

So far as my experience goes, only a good camera can take an accurate photograph of the hand and in it the bulging of the mounts of different planets can be seen. Besides, even the camera and all the lines can be seen in the photograph which help in forecasting correctly. In my opinion, those who want accurate forecasting should send photographs of their hands.

Where the facility of taking photographs is not available, they can send their hand-prints also but while doing so, they should take the following precautions:

The paper should be white and not rough. It should neither be thin nor be very smooth. It should not absorb ink.

While taking the print on the paper, it should be seen that the paper is quite sufficient in length and breadth. No portion of the hand should be kept out of the paper.

Before taking print, the hand should be washed with soap and there should be no ring on the finger.

Methods

In the following lines, I am introducing three or four methods of taking hand-prints clearly:

1. Taking Prints Through Smoke : Take a white (a bit hard) paper. It should be big enough to have space all around even after placing the full hand on it. Take a tablet of camphor in a utensil and ignite the tablet so that it burns and emits smoke. Put the paper over the utensil, taking care that it does not burn but only blackens. Move the paper to and fro to have good blackening. Let a thick coat of black smoke stick to the paper. The black coating should be uniform on the paper. If one tablet burns up, then add another to it. One should keep at least 10 or 12 camphor tablets at hand.

When the rear portion of the paper is completely blackened then put it on a fat and smooth table. There should be no cloth over the table. The surface of the table should be smooth and hard. The black part of the paper should remain upwards.

Now spread your hand and put it over the paper. Keep the fact in view that the impression of all the fingers of your hand and even the bracelets is on the paper. Now press your hand so that the impression of all the lines is transferred to the paper.

Now remove your hand straight off. You will see that the impression of even the smallest lines of your hand has been imprinted on the paper. If you can put a small handkerchief under the paper in its centre so that your palm comes just over it, then the impression recorded will be fool proof.

On a corner of this paper, write your name, address, date of birth and date of taking the hand-print. Put a white paper covering the hand-print so that the impressions do not mix up. Now fold it carefully and send it to a palmist for his study and get your forecast.

2. Taking Hand-print with the Help of Press Ink : In a printing press, there is big roller containing ink. When the books or materials have been printed, the ink on the roller fades. We should use this faded ink from the roller.

Spread a big white paper on a table then put a handkerchief under the paper. Now put your right hand on the roller and see that the whole of your palm is smeared with ink. When you are sure that the whole of your hand contains ink then put your hand cautiously over the paper and press it. Take the precaution that the centre of the palm is over the handkerchief so that a correct print is available.

When you have fixed your hand on the paper, press the joints with the other hand. Now remove your hand without moving it. You will see that the lines of your hand have been printed on the paper.

In the similar way, take a print of your left hand. Let the ink dry up for a few minutes. When it has dried, write your name, address and date of birth on the paper and send it to the palmist for his predictions.

In my opinion, everyone should send at least three sets of handprints so that one may know the handicaps of one hand-print in the other ones.

3. Hand-prints with Ink-pads : The ink pads used for putting rubber stamps are easily available. So one can easily get hand-prints by using ink-pad. The procedure of taking down hand-prints through this method is the same as that of taking down hand-prints through press-ink.

4. Hand-prints Through Photographs : This method is more accurate and authentic. One has to select an expert photographer for it. Care should also be taken that the light is neither too much so as to hide the smaller line of the hand in its brightness nor should it be so less that the smaller lines don't find their impression in the photograph. The photographer should be told beforehand about all this. The photograph should contain the impressions of the whole hand upto the wrist lines. The fingers should be separated while getting the photograph and they should not be attached or joined with each other.

The quality of the paper of the photograph should be very good. There can be one photograph of both the hands. It should not, however, be smaller than a post-card-sized photo.

By employing any of the above methods, an individual can get the hand-prints of his/her hands. These hand-prints can be sent to any fortune-teller, so that he may find out one's future accurately.

The Goddess Panchanguli

In the previous chapter, I have given a detailed account of the different lines and mounts found in the hand but I have not been able to give any information regarding the Goddess Panchanguli. In this chapter, I am giving in brief the information regarding it. One who wants to study in detail in this regard, should read my book Palmistry and the Devotion to the Panchanguli.

We get information regarding Panchanguli in many ancient books and it has been described there that one who worships Goddess Panchanguli regularly, becomes a successful fortune-teller very quickly. If he sees the hand of any person, he can presently find the past, the present and the future of that man. He also becomes aware of the minutest mysteries of the life of the man.

It is obvious that the famous western palmist, Cheiro also worshipped Goddess Panchanguli. Cheiro had lived in India for three years and had studied the worshipping of Goddess Panchanguli from an ascetic. He became famous due to this worshipping. I personally feel that the worshipping of Goddess Panchanguli provides a complete knowledge about the lines of the hand to an individual. In its worshipping, an auspicious time is a must.

The Month

This worshipping can be started in any month but Vaishakh, Kartik, Ashwin and Magh months are considered more auspicious.

The Date

This worshipping can be started on the second, the fifth, the seventh, the eighth, the tenth or the fifteenth day of the bright half of the Moon.

The Day

Sunday, Wednesday, Thursday and Friday are considered the best to start this worshipping.

The Nakshtra

Kritika, Rohini, Poonarvasu, Hast, all the three Uttaras, Anuradha and Shravana Nakshtras are considered favourable for it.

The Lagna

Sthir, Vrish, Singh, Vrishchik and Kumbh.

The Place

Pilgrims' place, confluence of the Ganges and the Yamuna, bank of a river, cave of a mountain and temple of Skandh are auspicious places. But if these places are not easily available, then a lonely room of the house can be used.

The Panchanguli Yantra

In any Tantra worship, one needs (if necessary) a yantra to be used. To accomplish the worshipping of Panchanguli, one needs a picture of Goddess Panchanguli and consecrated and life-imparted Panchanguli yantra. If one contacts our centre, such a yantra and picture can be sent to the desirous one.

Articles for Worshipping

Kumkum Yagyopaveet Black pepper

Abir Fruit Honey

Gulal Coconuts Ittar (scent)

Mauri Rice Deepak

Suparis (nuts) Almonds (earthen lamp)

Kesar (saffron) Cashewnuts Curd

Batasha Dried Grapes Sugar

Milk Prasad Mishri Betels

Camphor Incense Bhoja Patra

Ilaychi Loung Peepal leaves

Ghee Flowers Milk (unboiled)

Ganga water Well-water Garland of flowers

Some ‘Musts' to be Observed

No co-habitation and no talks about women.

No shaving.

Daily worship of Sandhya & Gayatri should be done.

One cannot worship when one is naked, has not bathed, has impious hands and has a cloth over head.

One cannot talk with anyone till one finishes one's bead count.

If one sneezes or passes out foul air from anus, then one should wash one's hands and touch the holy water to the ears.

During the period of prayer, one should abstain from sneezing, laziness, yawning, sleeping, tiredness, fear, impious clothing, talking and being angry.

One has to always do the same amount of prayer which one does on the very first day. It should neither be lessened nor be increased.

During the period of prayer if one goes to the lavatory for easement, then one should again take a bath and then start praying.

Rules to be Followed During the Period of Prayer

One should observe the following rules during the period of prayer:

Sleeping on the ground.

Leading a celebate's life.

Daily bathing.

Keeping silent.

Giving alms.

Serving the teacher.

Not performing sinful deeds.

Daily worshipping.

Worshipping and praying Gods.

Having faith in one's teacher and tutelary deity.

Regularity in the prayer.

Holiness.

Now in the following pages, I am describing the Panchanguli Mantra and the Kaal Gyan Mantra. Along with it, I am clarifying the solemn vow (Sankalpa). First of all, the worshipper should make a solemn vow, then he should recite the Kaal Gyan Mantra five times and then, before the Panchanguli yantra, meditating about the Panchanguli, he should recite the Panchanguli Mantra for one hundred and eight times. In the last, he should end his meditation on Panchanguli.

By following this ritual for sixty days, one can accomplish Panchanguli Sadhana Mantra or by praying one lakh Kaal Gyan Mantra, one can accomplish to know the past and the present of an incumbent.

For the facility of my readers, I am giving here the solemn vow (Sankalpa), the Panchanguli Dhyan Mantra and the Kaal Gyan Mantra respectively.

Introduction of Hands

All the individuals of the universe have something characteristic of their own in their hands. But it has been experienced that some types of people have something in common in their palms. In the following lines, I am giving common details of certain categories of people who are from different sections of the society:

1. Businessman : Those who are engaged in any trade or business, their thumbs are straight but inclined to their back a little. The head line in their palm is straight and clear and the mount of Mercury is generally protruded. It is also to be remembered that a successful businessman has no net on the mount of Mercury. His little finger is a bit longer.

Businessman

If any line is coming from the head line towards the mount of Mercury, then it is to be understood that the person is wholly successful and prosperous in his area of work. Also a person who has his fingers bigger than his palm, lacks nothing in his profession.

2. A Wealthy Man (Millionaire) : That person whose Sun line is strong and whose mount of Venus is developed and does not contain net or any broken line, is strong from economic point of view. But when the fate line is straight, clear and reddish and one of its branches is going towards the Sun line and the head line is fully developed, then certainly that man is rich. He has quite favourable conditions economically and enjoys full happiness.

Millionaire

3. An Accountant : The special feature in the hand of an accountant is that his mount of Mercury is developed and protruded. His mount of Sun is also protruded and on it the Sun line can be seen completely. His fate line should also be studied. If the fate line is reaching successfully the mount of Saturn at the root of the finger of Saturn without any break, then the person is a successful accountant.

Accountant

If the above facts are available and the finger of Saturn and the little finger are a bit longer than ordinary, along with it if his thumbs are strong, then he gets an important post in a bank. Such a person can be an income tax officer also.

Along with these signs, if the Moon line is fully developed and the fate line also helps, then the person becomes a chartered accountant.

4. Judge : If in a person, who has his index finger longer than the finger of Apollo, the little finger reaches above the third phalange of the finger of Apollo, all his fingers are beautiful and has a fully developed mount of Jupiter with a cross mark, then such a person is a famous advocate.

Judge

Along with all the above characteristics if the person has a faultless, thin and clear fate line, the Sun is fully influential and the thumb is long and leans back, then the person is certainly a successful Judge.

With the above characteristics, if any branch of the fate line goes towards the mount of Jupiter, then the person certainly becomes the Chief Justice.

5. Sculptor : If the fingers of the hand are long and their tops are rectangular, the Sun line is fully developed, clear and deep, there is no obstructing line on the mount of Saturn and has a thin and long thumb, then that man is surely a successful sculptor.

sculptor

6. Soldier : The person who is quite healthy, strongly built and tall, also whose hands are quite long, then we find the symptoms of a soldier in him.

Soldier

If his palm has a bigger area of Mars and it has a shining star; also if his fate line is developed and clear, then that person is certainly a soldier.

If the person has both the Mars fully developed and the Sun line is faultless and clearly present on the mount of Sun and also if his thumb is strong, long and inclined backward, then the person is certainly either a General or aBrigadier.

Along with the above characteristics, if the person has a triangle on the Sun line and all the fingers have full length, then such a person certainly becomes the chief of the army staff.

7. I.A.S. : An I.A.S. functions on important posts of a Collector, Secretary or other administrative posts. Such persons are very helpful in administration.

I.A.S.

Those who have their little finger larger, i.e., its top has reached a bit further of the third phalange of the finger of Apollo; along with it the Sun line is of quite high quality, then those persons become I.A.S. officers.

If the Sun line is weak or broken then the person becomes just an I.A.S. officer.

If the little finger is unusually long, the Sun line is prominent and the head line is fully developed, along with these, if his fate line is long, faultless and full, then the incumbent gets a high assignment in the Central Government.

If the fate line and the mount of Jupiter are perfect, then certainly the man becomes a secretary or high official in the Central Government. His works influence the administration.

8. Sailor : Those who serve on boats, ships or those who are high officials in the Navy are being given the nomenclature of sailors by me. An expert navy official has a full length hand and has a fully developed mount of Moon. That person whose mount of Moon is fully developed and from the mount of Moon, a line goes towards the Sun and the fate line and head line are fully developed, then that man is sure to be the Chief of the Naval Staff.

Sailor

If the mount of Moon is less developed or the Moon line is weak and rest all the characteristics are there in the palm, then he holds a high post in the Navy.

If the mount of the Moon is completely depressed and the fate line is weak, then he just becomes a boatman or a sailor.

9. Doctor : The person who has a fully developed Mercury and the little finger has full length, i.e., its top reaches half of the top phalange of the finger of Apollo and there are three vertical lines on the regions of Mars, then he becomes a successful doctor.

Doctor

If the above characteristics are there and along with it the mount of Mars is fully developed and the line of Mars is also strong and prominent, then he becomes a successful surgeon.

If the mount of Mars is depressed but all the rest of the characteristics of the palm are available and the mount of Jupiter is developed and it contains a sign of square on it, then that person becomes a famous physician.

10. Engineer : By engineer, I mean to suggest a scientist and a mechanic too. If all the fingers of a person have full length, the mount of Saturn is developed and on it the line of fate has stood over faultlessly and the mount contains three or four vertical lines, then that man becomes a successful scientist.

Engineer

If the above characteristics are there but the mount of Jupiter is weak, then he becomes a successful engineer but if his mount of Moon is prominent, then he becomes an engineer in the waterworks.

If the above signs are there in the palm and the head line and the line of Saturn are fully developed, then he becomes a successful pilot but he should have the best mount of Moon and the Moon line.

11. Religious Preacher : If a person has the index finger longer than the finger of Apollo and the mount of Jupiter is developed and has a sign of cross over it, then surely that man becomes a successful priest or a religious man.

Religious Preacher

Along with the above mentioned characteristics if he has a fully developed Sun line and the mount of Sun, then that person becomes a preacher or religious preceptor.

If the above characteristics exist and the life line is broken, then certainly that man will become an ascetic and he gets honour and respect only after becoming an ascetic.

12. Artist : When the palms of a person have full length and are without knots, his fingers have slope and the tops of the fingers are sharp, then the person is an artist.

Artist

Along with the above characteristics, if the mount of Moon is developed, then the person is considered the best of the artists. If the mount of Mercury is developed and the Mercury line is in full length and faultlessly enlarged, then the person becomes a successful musician. Along with the above characteristics if the mount of Venus is fully developed and it has an enlarged region, then the person becomes a successful and famous dancer.

Along with it if the fate line and the Sun line are clear, deep and reddish then he will be more successful, popular and famous in his field.

13. Writer : If the mount of Jupiter and the mount of Moon are fully developed and the finger of Sun is longer than the index finger and the fingers are not knotty, then the person is a successful writer.

Writer

Along with these, if the Sun line is long, faultless and the mount of Sun is at its ususal place, then he becomes famous through his writing.

If the above characteristics are there and the line of Moon is moving towards the mount of Mercury in a bow shape, then that person becomes a successful poet.

Along with these, if the head line and the fate line are fully developed, then that person certainly gets respect, honour and fame in his/her area.

14. Actor/Actress : If all the fingers of the hand are soft and slopy, the Sun finger is specially long and is pointed and the fate line is of full length, then the person is a successful actor or actress.

Actor/Actress

If the Sun line is specially fine, long, clear, soft and faultless in its length, then that person becomes a matchless artist.

If there is a sign of star on the Sun line, then that actor or actress gains world-fame.

15. Lascivious Hand : If the area of the Venus is badly shaped, is protruded more than necessary, is very smooth and risened; along with it the phalange of thumb is much longer and there is a sign of star on the mount of Venus, then the person is very lascivious.

Lascivious Hand

If along with the above, the person has a point or a cross on the line of Saturn, then he gets notoriety in this field.

16. Murderer : If the palm lacks the heart line and the ring of the Venus is dual, the hand is strong and hard, the thumb is short, thick and fat, then the person is certainly a murderer.

Murderer

Along with these, if the heart line, the head line and the life line join on the mount of Mars, then the person is surely a cruel murderer and will do more than one murders in his life.

17. Spy : If the palm has a developed mount of Saturn and the mount of Mercury is depressed but the fate line is of full length and there is a mark of triangle on the mount of Mars, then the person is a successful spy.

Spy

18. Teacher : One whose hand has a developed life line, fate line and Sun line and the mount of Jupiter is also developed, there is a mark of cross over it and the index finger is longer than the finger of Apollo, then that person is a successful teacher or lecturer.

Teacher

The Different Yogas (Combinations) of Palmistry

Gaj Laxmi Yog

Definition : If, in both the hands of an incumbent, the fate line starting from the bracelets, goes to the mount of Saturn and the mount of Sun is developed having a thin, long and reddish Sun line; along with these signs if the head line, health line and fate line are prominent, then his/her palm has Gaj Laxmi Yog.

Result : One who is bestowed with this yog, though born in a very ordinary family, gets very high respect. These persons are also known from their works. Economically and materially such persons lack nothing. Such persons are gentle by nature, judicious and virtuous. By engaging themselves in trade or working in foreign countries, they get much success. A person having Gaj Laxmi Yog is wholly successful in his life. His fame in his field remains permanent even after his death.

Gaj Laxmi Yog

Note : It is to be observed that in this yog the above situations should be found in both the hands. If it is to be found in only one hand, then the result will be half of that predicted above.

Amla Yog

Definition : If the mount of Moon is developed—the mounts of the Sun and Venus are also quite bulging and the Moon line goes towards the mount of Mercury, then the person has Amla Yog.

Result : A person having Amla Yog is considered wise, clever and famous. Such a person is wholly successful economically and materially. He goes abroad in his life on business tours. If the mount of Venus is fully developed in his hand and on it there are no obstructing lines, then certainly he enjoys material happiness. He has illicit relationship with other women also. He does not need to face blame in his life.

Amla Yog

Shubh Yog or Auspicious Combination

Definition : If the right hand has a developed mount of Saturn and the fate line is there clearly, then the person has Shubh Yog.

Result : A person who has Shubh Yog in his palm becomes a famous orator and can fascinate the public. Such a person having this yog is capable of getting from the public whatever he desires. His speech is enchanting and he is adept in the art of getting favour from the public. Such a person has a facinating and splendid personality.

Note : A person bestowed with the ‘Shubh Yog' has a rise in fate away from his birth place.

Shubh Yog

Budh Yog or Business Combination

Definition : If the mount of Mercury is developed in itself in the right hand with a bow marked line reaching the mount of Mercury from the mount of Moon, and this line is not broken or chained or is not very thick, then it marks Budh Yog in a person's hand.

Result : One who has the Budh Yog, gets success through business. He never lacks wealth or honour. Physically, he keeps ft and looks attractive. He spreads his business abroad by his efforts. Such a person takes quick decisions and has a healthy mind. He is very clever and wise and destroys his enemies.

Budh Yug

Note : It is to be noted that in this yog the person has a developed mount of Mercury. It should be reddish and should be on its own position. It should neither be out of the palm nor it should be inclined towards the Sun.

Indra Yog

Definition : A person who has a naturally developed mount of Mars and the head line and the fate line are of full length, straight and clear, then that person has Indra Yog in his palm.

Result : A person who has Indra Yog in his hand, is strong, clever and successful in warfare. Such a person gets a high post in Military or Police and leads the life of a king. Such a person is clever in conversation and has affable and simple nature. His fate rises after the twenty-eighth year of his life.

Indra Yug

Note : This is like Raj Yog, so it has been experienced that the person who has this yog, dies prematurely. But such a person becomes famous in the short span of his life. His honour spreads far and wide. Materially, such people do not lack anything. Such persons get full respect, reputation and fame in their lives.

Marut Yog

Definition : If the mount of Venus is fully developed and it has no obstructing lines, the mount of Jupiter is also very clear and has a mark of cross on it and the Moon line is strong, straight and clear, then such a person has Marut Yog.

Result : Person having Marut Yog is well conversant in the art of conversation and is very able. He is large-hearted, takes pleasure in helping others. Economically such persons remain happy and successful. They spread their business by their efforts. Such persons take quick decisions and know and recognise the value of time correctly. Such persons can claim whatever they wish for.

Marut Yog

Note : While studying this yog it should be taken care that the mounts of Venus, Jupiter and Moon are fully developed in themselves and the lines of Venus and Moon do not fall short in any way.

Lagnadhi Yog

Definition : If the fate line is fully developed, all the mounts in the palm are also developed in themselves and the line of Mercury has its full length, then the person has Lagnadhi Yog.

Result : A person having Lagnadhi Yog is very learned and a clever speaker. Others also recognise his learning. From health point of view, the person is healthy, strong and atttractive. He has high and sublime thoughts in his heart. He has no interest in worldly affairs and tricks. He becomes famous by his works.

Lagnadhi Yog

Note : This yog will be recognised only when all the mounts are fully developed and are situated at their respective places. If the mounts are scattered here and there, then the person does not get the benefit of this yog.

Adhi Yog

Definition : If the line of Moon is developed and reaches the mount of Mercury and one of its branches touches the mount of Saturn, then the person has Adhi Yog.

Result : A person born with this combination has humility. He is clever, cautious and works according to the need of the time. Such a person enjoys his whole life happily. He lacks nothing materially. But sometimes such persons believe on their enemies and are later cheated by them.

Adhi Yog

Note : This combination emphasises on the line of Moon and the point to be considered is that the inclination of the line should be towards the mount of Mercury but one branch of it should reach the other mount.

Shakat Yog or Unfortunate Combination

Definition : If the mounts of Saturn and Moon are depressed, the mount of Venus has vertical and horizontal lines and the fate line is very weak, then the person has Shakat Yog.

Result : A person, who is born with Shakat Yog, remains unfortunate throughout his life. He keeps on struggling throughout his life. Many times, he has got to take loan to pull through. Such persons do not have any reputation in the society and they lead a very ordinary life.

Shakat Yog

Note : Persons having Shakat Yog get the above mentioned results mostly. But if his mount of Jupiter and mount of Mercury are developed and the mount of Jupiter has a cross mark, then he does not get such a bad result as mentioned above.

Daridra Yog or Combination of Poverty

Definition : If all the mounts in the palm of a person are weak, the mount of Moon has points and the little finger has a sign of a star then it amounts to Daridra Yog.

Result : A person born with Daridra Yog combination spends the whole of his wealth by his evil doings, though he is born in the house of a multi-millionaire. He is compelled to lead the life of a very poor man. The whole of his life becomes very ordinary in all aspects.

Daridra Yog

Note : Palmists have also mentioned the following combinations as Daridra Yog. These are besides the above mentioned combination:

If the Sun line and the fate line are very weak and broken.

If the fate line is thick and inset in the palm and reaches upto the first phalange of the finger of Saturn.

If the mount of Venus is depressed and contains more than necessary obstructing lines.

If a number of lines are descending down in the palm.

If there is a mark of star on the first phalange of the finger of Saturn and the fate line is weak.

If the line of Sun has turned and is going towards the mount of Venus.

If the palm is big and heavy and all the mounts on it are weak and depressed.

If the mount of Moon is weak and contains more than one crosses.

If the fingers of the hand are short, their tops are squarish and the fate line is moving out of the palm.

Any of the above combinations makes a person weak economically.

Durdhara Yog

Definition : If the mount of Venus, the mount of Sun and the mount of Saturn are depressed and the Sun line is broken, then it makes Durdhara Yog.

Result : A person having Durdhara Yog spends his early life with great difficulty but his fate rises after the thirty-sixth year of his life and after this age, he progresses miraculously.

Durdhara Yog

Note : The results of this combination are very strange. The first thirty-six years are spent in great misery, difficulty and tension but the age after 36th year is full of happiness and prosperity. He earns wealth, reputation, fame and respect in full after this age.

Kemdrum Yog

Definition : If the mount of Sun and the mount of Venus are undeveloped and the Sun line is broken, then such a combination in the palm is called Kemdrum Yog.

Result : One who is born with this combination always remains miserable. He may have other good combinations but this combination destroys all other good combinations. Such a person commits mistakes himself and is not far-sighted. He repents later on. Economically, he remains very poor and changes the means of his livelihood frequently. Such a person always depends upon others.

Kemdrum Yog

Note : Some learned palmists think that if the fate line is prominent, then it destroys the Kemdrum Yog. Some other eminent persons have clarified other facts which break the Kemdrum Yog.

The following situations in the palm can lessen the influence of Kemdrum in the life of a person:

If the Sun line is fully strong, clear and straight.

If a branch line from the Sun line reaches the mount of Mercury.

The mount of Venus is prominent and is enlarged in area and it has no other lines or net.

The mount of Moon is fully developed and a line coming out of it reaches the mount of Mercury.

There are four full and clear bracelets.

Special Note : In fact, the combination of Kemdrum is full of misery. It has been written in the book entitled Samudrik Sudha that if a person has full Rajya Yog and prominent rise in fate but if he has Kemdrum Yog, all the other good combinations are destroyed and those auspicious combinations also start giving results of inauspicious combinations.

Anfa Yog

Definition : A person who has a wide and influencial Venus area and the Sun line and the fate line establish relationship ahead, then such a combination is called ‘Anfa Yog'.

Result : A person having Anfa Yog has a very impressive and magnetic personality. He is able to influence others with his personality. He is honoured in the society by his good works. Such a person adheres to his own thinking. He is wise, virtuous and gentle. He wants to be honoured and gives respect to others.

Anfa Yog

Note : This combination is there because of the union of the Venus, the Sun and the Saturn. So, with the influence of different planets, its results also change. If there is the result of more than one planet, then it should be considered a mixed result.

The Venus

If there is Anfa Yog in the palm and the mount of Venus and the line of Venus are the most developed, then that man will be a famous lover. He comes into contact with many other women along with his wife. Such a man can enchant the hearts of others.

The Saturn

If there is Anfa Yog in the palm and the mount of Saturn is fully developed and line of Saturn is also influential, then such a man is very lucky. He is born in a famous family. He leads his life happily. He is able to influence others by his words. He knows it fully well how to spell-bound people by his dialogues. He gets the facility of vehicle throughout his life. He also keeps contact with fair ladies one way or the other. Such a person is virtuous, wealthy and bestowed with a son.

The Sun

If there is Anfa Yog in the palm and the mount of Sun and the Sun line are prominent and strong, then the person has special interest in business. He is also adept in poetry, music and other fine arts. Such a person gets special success in service and very soon, he is able to get a higher post. His body remains healthy and attractive. His personality influences all those who meet him. He becomes very popular in the society because of it. Such a person remains happy and successful in his life. He spends his life happily and is honoured in the society.

Sunfa Yog

If the mounts of the Sun, the Saturn and the Mercury are developed and the three respective lines are clear and unbroken, then the person has Sunfa Yog.

Result : The person who has Sunfa Yog is laborious in his life. He earns wealth due to his labour and makes a respectable position in the society. He exhibits caution and cleverness in all his works. Being farsighted, all his decisions are correct. Such a person leads a fully happy life.

Sunfa Yog

Note : This yog is possible due to the Sun, the Saturn and the Mercury. The one prior condition for this yog is that the line of Mercury, the line of Saturn and the line of Sun should have relationship in the palm at one place. In the following lines, I am giving in detail the special results affected by each planet.

The Mercury

If there is the Sunfa Yog in the palm and the line of Mercury is more developed and more clear than any other line, then the person takes more interest in arts. Such a person follows the social traditions and responsibilities fully. Also such a person follows the social taboos. He is wise, handsome and a well-wisher of others.

The Saturn

If there is Sunfa Yog in the palm and the mount of Saturn and the Saturn line are specially prominent and straight, then the person is considered wise and gets success in politics. Such a person does not worry about wealth in his life. He can keep secrets and does not reveal them till he gains his point. His behaviour is simple, straight and mysterious.

The Sun

If there is Sunfa Yog in the palm but the mount of Sun is specially developed and the line of Sun is prominent, then that person becomes an honoured high officer. He is very successful in the field of administration. He is considered well-versed in settling responsible works. Such persons start from an ordinary post but are able to gain a higher post successfully.

Ashubh Yog or Inauspicious Combination

If the sun line is broken and there is no mount of the Sun, then it is called Ashubh Yog in the palm.

Result : A person having Ashubh Yog is lascivious and full of wrath. He is compelled to lead an ordinary life. He believes in earning by cheating others. Such a person is wholly unsuccessful in life and faces difficulties at every step.

Ashubh Yog

Shubh Kartari Yog or Auspicious Actions Combination

Definition : If the palm has depth in its centre and the mount of Sun and the mount of Jupiter are protruded, then the person has a combination of Shubh Kartari Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, leads an influential and glorious life. He has more than one source of his income. He is able to collect wealth. Such a person is attractive, strong and healthy physically.

Shubh Kartari Yog

Pap Kartari Yog or Combination of Sinful Acts

Definition : If the palm is overset and the mount of Sun and Saturn are suppressed or weak, then it is called as Pap Kartari Yog.

Result : A person born with this yog always indulges in sinful acts. He gets pleasure in cheating others or putting them to loss. Such a person is callous-hearted and is compelled to lead a life of misery and poverty.

Pap Kartari Yog

Ubhay Charik Yog

Definition : If there is a mark of square on the mount of Sun and the top part of the finger of Mercury reaches the last phalange of the finger of Apollo, then the person has Ubhay Charik Yog.

Result : A person, who has this yog, is far-sighted as well as a man who understands the circumstances fully well. Such a person is just and is honoured in the country for his objectiveness. Such a man is firm, strong, healthy and is firm to his words. If he gives words to anyone then he tries to fulfil his promises.

Ubhay Charik Yog

Note : It is necessary to have a full square on the mount of the Sun in this combination. The smaller the square, the more auspicious and favourable the result is.

Parvat Yog or Combination of Mountains

Definition : If the fate line, starting from the bracelets, goes straight to the mount of Saturn and this line is thin, deep, clear and unbroken and forms the shape of a fish at its origin, then the person has Parvat Yog.

Result : One who has parvat in his hand, is a self-made man. His fate helps him in whatever he engages himself to do and he gets success in those tasks. He gets full success in the field of education and in his youth, he helps the poor, the orphan and the suffering. I have experienced that the person has all the above virtues but he is also lascivious. He has illegitimate relationship with many women besides his own wife. He gets praise in the society and lacks nothing in his life.

Parvat Yog

Note : It has been written in the books of palmistry that if such a person tries, he can become a Sarpanch (village-headman) or a Municipal Commissioner or member of the District Board. Though such persons are not successful politicians but they can be faithful to many high ranking ministers or members of the legislative assembly.

Wasi Yog

Definition : If the palm has the ring of Saturn and ring of the Mercury, then it is Wasi Yog.

Result : A person, who has Wasi Yog, is expert and clever in his work. Such a person is always smiling, joyful, virtuous and clever. All of his works speak eloquently of his cleverness. Such a person does not lack anything from the family side. Firstly, no enemy dares to come near him; if at all he comes, he himself gets yielded.

Wasi Yog

If the palm has Wasi Yog but the fate line is broken, then the person commits many such mistakes for which he remains repentant and perturbed in his life. He always thinks of taking revenge and collecting money through cheating is his tendency throughout his life.

If the Sun line is broken or weak or there is no Sun line, then the person is compelled to lead a very ordinary and miserable life.

Note : While studying Wasi Yog, one should also consider the Sun line and the line of Saturn. Only after studying them should one venture to predict his results about the Wasi Yog.

Veshi Yog

Definition : If the palm has the ring of Mercury and the ring of Saturn, but at the same time, there is a girdle of Venus, then there is Veshi Yog.

Result : A person who is born with this combination is simple in nature, clever, patient and sober. He has the ability to influence others by his conversation. Such a person is expert in leading people. But he always lacks economically. Though he earns lakhs, he also spends lakhs because of his excessive expenditure. It has been observed that such a person does not have any bank balance. Even then he is honoured in the society and many people follow his orders.

Veshi Yog

Bhaskar Yog or The Sun Combination

Definition : If the Sun line has a relationship with the line of Mercury, the line of Mercury has it with the line of Moon, and the mount of Jupiter and the line of Jupiter are long and prominent, then the Bhaskar Yog is there.

Result : A person born with Bhaskar Yog combination is fully successful economically. He never lacks anything materially. He knows more than one art and so the sources of his income are also more than one. Physically, such a person is well-built and has an influential personality. His enemies always fear him and he is well-versed in making friends. He has interest in literature, music and arts. He gives money and respect to artists and thus gains respect, honour and fame in the society. Such a person has a magnetic attraction by virtue of which he has a galaxy of male and female friends.

Bhaskar Yog

Note : It is necessary in this combination that the mounts of

Jupiter, Sun Mercury and Moon are fully developed. Along with these, the Sun line and the line of Mercury should be faultless and should join each other. Similarly, the line of Moon should contact the line of Mercury but the Sun line should never touch the line of Moon.

Gandharva Yog or Combination of Musicians

Definition : If the mount of Venus in both the hands is fully developed and it has a mark of square on it, with the Sun line and the mount of Sun as prominent and clear, then it is called Gandharva Yog.

Gandharva Yog

Result : A person who has Gandharva Yog, is a famous musician or singer. Such persons devote the whole of their lives in accomplishing arts and get complete success in the field. They also become famous abroad by means of arts. They do not lack wealth or reputation and fulfil all their material desires.

Note : While studying this combination, it is to be remembered that the mount of Sun and the Sun line are fully developed and correct. Besides, the mount of Venus should also be developed in both the hands. Such persons do not have any economic obstructions.

Vasumati Yog

Definition : If the fingers are without knots and long and the mounts of Sun, Saturn, Jupiter and Mercury are developed at their own places, then it is called as Vasumati Yog.

Result : Such a person enjoys complete material happiness in his life. He never lacks wealth in his life. Such a person earns wealth by his own efforts and earns reputation and fame in the society.

Vasumati Yog

Note : It is necessary in this combination that the mounts of Jupiter, Mercury, Sun and Saturn are developed and are not away from their respective places. Only then the Vasumati Yog is possible.

Parashachatussagar Yog

Definition : If the mounts of Sun, Mercury, Jupiter and Saturn are suppressed and all the mounts are away from their places, then the Parashachatussagar Yog is there.

Result : One who has such a combination, always suffers economically. He also does not get mental satisfaction and comfort. The more efforts he makes, the more are his difficulties. Such a person leads his life in poverty.

Parashachatussagar Yog

Chatussagar Yog

Definition : If all the mounts of the palm are developed and all the lines are prominent, straight, simple and clear, then such a hand has Chatussagar Yog.

Result : Persons who have this yog become famous by their own works. They also get success in leading the society and the nation by their own efforts. Such persons are successful and happy economically. They have a forceful voice and by their speeches, they can influence people. Such persons go abroad by their efforts and earn fame in foreign countries. If such persons are in government service, then their service calls them to go on journeys and they get success in them. These people are quite satisfied with their families.

Chatussagar Yog

Note : Dragon's Tail and Dragon's Head are not considered in this yog–all the other mounts should be fully developed and they should not have points nor nets. Besides, the palm of the person should be flexible, tender and pink in colour.

Rog Yog or Combination for Diseases

Definition : According to the learned palmists, if the following combinations are found in the palms, then it mounts to Rog Yog:

Rog Yog

Weak Physique: The nails are thin, the life line is faint and having chains, and is inclined below.

Premature Death : The heart line touches the mount of Saturn, besides, if there is a cross mark in the middle of the head line and the life line is broken.

Weakness in Childhood : The life line is very thick and has become full of chains below the mount of Jupiter.

Hereditary Disease : There is a mark of island at the place from where the life line starts, then the person has hereditary disease.

Weak Body : The life line is like a stair and broken.

Accident in Childhood : There is a point in the beginning of the life line.

Undeveloped Body : Many branches come out of the middle of the life line and proceed in the region below.

Weakness in Old Age : The health line is wide and in the end, it has become a bunch or has broken.

Sudden Death : The heart line goes below the mount of Saturn and then appears suddenly or if the head line touches the bracelets.

Assassination : The life line, head line and the heart line are joined completely at the beginning and have become full of chains, later.

Accident because of an Animal : The head line has suddenly broken below the mount of Sun or the mount of Saturn.

Head Injury : There is a mark of red colour on the head line.

Terrible Accident in Youth : The Head line is inclined below and is wayward in appearance.

Hanging : The life line has suddenly ended and there is a mark of cross or of two stars in the triangle formed by the joining of the heart line, head line and the life line.

Imprisonment : Many lines are issuing out at the beginning of the fate line and are going below, and the fate line has gone upto the second part of the line of Saturn.

Drowning : There is a mark of circle on the mount of Moon or there is a triangle on the mount of Moon, or there is a point or a triangle or a star on the mount of Mercury.

Death during Travelling : The travelling lines are broken and are wider than necessary.

Cancer : The line of Mars in both the hands has branches or there is a mark of Star in the beginning of the life line.

Epilepsy : There is a cross over the heart line or the health line appears of different colours.

Paralysis : There are two vertical lines on the heart line or the mount of Moon.

Mental Diseases : The head line is wayward or like a staircase and it is leaning towards the health line or the head line is joined with the health line, or there is a cluster of lines at the point of their meeting.

Asthma : The hand is soft and there is sign of an island on the life line.

Blindness : There is a square on the life line or there is a spot on the heart line or there is a cross on the heart line or on the health line, or there is a big black spot at the starting of the Sun line.

Mental Disease : The head line is more than enough wide and there is a black mark on it.

Anaemia : The heart line is wide and weak and the nails are a little bluish in colour.

Colds : The mount of Moon is very developed and protruded.

Heart Disease : There are two heart lines which join each other in between or the heart line reaches the mount of Saturn.

Arthritis or Gout : The mount of Moon is fully developed and the whole of it is being cut by a single line or the mount is divided into two by it, or there is a cluster of lines at the end of the life line.

Unconsciousness : The health line starts from the life line and the head line is proceeding towards the heart line, then the person has repeated fits of unconsciousness.

Deafness : The mount of Jupiter is weak and below it, there is a point on the head line.

Tuberculosis : The nails are long, thin and curved and there are many small islands on the head line.

Dropsy : The mount of Moon is excessively protruded and it has more than one crosses or points on it.

Trembling : The head line has a black mark and the life line has a star mark in the beginning, then the organs of a person tremble a little.

Diphtheria : The life line and the head line are broken and the mount of Saturn has a cross mark or a rectangle below it.

Obesity : The heart line is broken and the hand is fat and plump.

Hysteria : The hand is ordinary and there are marks of stars (more than one) on the mount of Moon.

Fatal Disease : The life line is broken and one part of it is related to the mount of Venus.

Indigestion : The head line is narrow and the health line is wayward.

Loses of Memory : The head line is broken at many places and the person has loss of memory.

Gases : Many lines come out of the life line and proceed below it.

Pleurisy : If a line coming out of the life line reaches the mount of Saturn and there is either a mark of a triangle or marks of two stars, then such a person suffers from pleurisy.

Murder : If there is a mark of a star on the first phalange of the finger of Apollo, then the person dies due to the shot of a bullet. This mark was clearly visible in the hand of Mahatma Gandhi.

Suicide : The first phalange of the second finger is quite long and there is a mark of star at the end of fate line.

Dental Diseases : If the heart line and the health line are wavy, then the person suffers from dental troubles throughout his life.

Diseases of the Private Organs : If there are very minute lines on the mount of Mars, then the person suffers from the diseases of the private organs.

Napunsak Yog or Combination of Impotency

Definition : The mount of Venus is suppressed and has a spot on it.

Napunsak Yog

Result : One who has such combination has weak chromosomes or dead chromosomes due to which he does not get any issue.

The Chandra-Mangal Yog or Combination of the Moon and the Mars

Definition : If the line of Moon proceeds to meet the Mars line or the fate line and the Moon line join faultlessly, then the person has Chandra-Mangal Yog.

Result : A person having such a yog is always happy and prosperous economically. He knows the art of earning. He behaves in a tricky way with his relatives and partners and has relationship with many women.

Chandra-Mangal Yog

Note : It is to be observed particularly in Chandra-Mangal Yog that both the lines are prominent and correct and that both the lines have joined but have not taken the shape of a chain.

Sati Yog

Definition : If in the palm of a woman, the mount of Jupiter is fully developed and all the fingers are long, beautiful and healthy, then the woman is very faithful to her husband.

Result : The woman who has this combination follows a true and devoted life towards her husband. She gives full cooperation and pleasure to her husband. She is a virtuous lady.

Sati Yog

Kulata Yog or Combination of Unfaithful Wife

Definition : Learned plamists have defined the following combinations as the Kulata Yog. Women who have these combinations are not faithful to their husbands. They have illicit relationships with other persons.

Kulata Yog

The mount of Venus is quite developed and the heart line is chained.

There are two parallel lines leaning over the heart line.

There is a mark of an island on the heart line.

There is a net on the mount of Venus and the heart line is extremely weak. Both the hands should have this combination.

There is a point in the triangle formed by the heart line, head line and health line.

There is neither the mount of Venus nor the mount of Moon in both the hands.

There is a mark of star on the thumb or on the first phalange of the thumb.

There is a mark of star on the first phalange of the index finger.

There are excessive horizontal and vertical lines on the mount of Venus.

There is a cross mark at the root of the thumb.

There is a mark of star on the mount of Venus.

Many lines come out at the end of the fate line and proceed below.

The heart line is enlarged and reaches the other end of the palm.

The heart line touches the phalanges of the finger.

The origin of the life line and the heart line are different and both the lines are wide.

The health line goes parallel to the Mercury line.

The mount of Venus is ordinarily developed, the fingers are soft and pointed.

There is a white mark on the heart line.

There is no cross on the mount of Jupiter but there is a cross on the mount of Venus.

The head line is leaning below and joins the life line proceeding ahead.

There is an island on the line of marriage.

The thumb is long and the heart line is dual.

The heart line is narrow and reaches the mount of Jupiter.

Smaller lines come out from the fate line and reach the heart line.

The heart line and the fate line join under the mount of Jupiter.

A line coming out of the mount of Venus reaches the mount of Mercury.

The heart line and the fate line join together and have become chained.

Lines coming out of the mount of Venus cut across the health line, the life line and the head line.

There are marks of two or three stars near the mount of Venus on the life line.

Two parallel lines go towards the mount of Moon from the mount of Venus.

There is a benedictory mark (Swastik) or there is a mark of half-moon on the mount of Moon.

The fate line has ended near the bracelets and there is a mark of star on the mount of Venus.

The fate line starts from the mount of Moon.

There is a mark of star on the second phalange of the thumb.

There is a shape of a rectangle in the palm and it contains a star mark.

The heart line and the head line join and form a mark of an arch.

Result : If any of the above-mentioned combinations is found in the palm of a man or a woman, then certainly that person has illicit relationship with a person of opposite sex but his love is mostly secret.

Akhand Samrajyapati Yog or A Sovereign's Combination

Definition : If in both the hands of a person, the fate line joins the mount of Saturn after forming a mark of fish at the bracelets, the Sun line is long, narrow and thin and a thin line coming out from the mount of Moon and touches the mount of Mercury and if the mounts of Sun, Saturn and Venus are fully developed, then the person has Akhand Samrajyapati Yog.

Result : A person who is born with this combination enjoys all sorts of pleasures in his life and becomes famous by his good works. Such a person is a high-level social reformer, leader of a nation, an expert of a particular field, courageous and brave.

Sati Yog

Note : It is essential for this combination that in both the hands the mounts of Sun, Saturn and Venus are developed and the line of Sun and the line of Saturn are clear and strong. The person's palm should be reddish and should be long and not wide. This combination is considered very important in the science of palmistry.

Shash Yog

Definition : There are three lines, on the first bracelet. The fate line makes a mark of a fish and proceeds to the point of the mount of Saturn and this mount is fully developed. Then it is called Shash Yog.

Result : One who has Shash or Shashak combination, rises very high, though he is born in a very ordinary family. He occupies a very important chair in politics and is considered an expert in politics. Such a person has servants and enjoys the amenities of vehicles. He is the village head or the municipal chairman or a great leader of the nation. Such a person is very simple and considerate. He has a happy combination of many virtues.

Shash Yog

Note : A great emphasis has been given to Saturn in this combination. So if the line of Saturn and the mount of Saturn are better, then the progress of the person is better. Such a person rises slowly but his progress is permanent.

Malavya Yog

Definition : If the mount of Venus is neither suppressed nor much developed but it is ordinarily developed, bright and healthy, along with the life line reaching the bracelet gives full extension to the mount of Venus and the thumb is long and inclined back with the mount of Venus with no interfering lines or any net at any point, then it is called Malavya Yog.

Result : A person having Malavya Yog is quite handsome and attractive. He has such an enchanting personality that he very soon attracts others towards himself.

Malavya Yog

Such person has attractive and handsome face, red colour, thin waist, long and beautiful nose, bright eyes, attractive personality and splendour of the Moon. Such a person is also intelligent and wise and does not fear even the toughest situations. Wealth is itself attracted towards him and without much labour, he has many sources of income. He has the facility of a vehicle for the whole of his life and enjoys all the happinesses of life. Such persons are highly civilized and get fame in their country and abroad by their works.

Note : The person who has Malavya Yog is originally an artist. If the mount of Venus and the line of Venus are prominent and healthy, then the person becomes famous in the field of poetry or music or dance. Such persons enter the field of art and get full success in it. They are kind and generous by nature. They do not lack anything related with Venus.

Hans Yog

Definition : If the index finger is taller than the finger of Apollo, the mount of Jupiter is fully developed and reddish and has only the mark of a cross and no other mark except that, then the person has Hans Yog.

Result : The person who has Hans Yog is tall, attractive and healthy. His personality is always blooming. Smiling face, broad forehead, pointed nose, broad chest and attractive personality are his special features. He is far-sighted and helps his friends, acquaintees and relatives. He behaves decently with all and gets a respectable position in the society. Such a person invariably gets a very high post.

Hans Yog

Note : This combination is due to Jupiter and it is considered one of the Panch Maha Purush Yog. Such persons are successful judges and get honour from the society for their impartial judgement. Such persons do not indulge in evil compromise under the influence of pressure or greed. Such persons are also very attractive. They have such characteristics in their personality that the number of their acquaintees are very great. They spend their old age very happily.

Ruchak Yog

Definition : If the mount of Mars is fully developed and reddish and the line of Mars is straight and beautiful, then the person has Ruchak Yog.

Result : A person born with this combination is strong and healthy, physically. He makes the name of the country and society famous by his efforts. At times, he leads the country. He spends his life like a king. He is always alive to his country, culture and to arts. He raises the honour of his country and does great efforts in this regard. Such a person's life and character are of the highest type. He never works under pressure. Economically, he lacks nothing. Such a person has longevity and gets a very high post in military or police. He gets honour and respect because of his ability of leadership and his practicability of actions.

Ruchak Yog

Note : In palmistry, this combination is also one of the ‘Pancha Maha Purush Yog'. Such persons are honoured in their own country and abroad and get full benefits.

Bhadra Yog

Definition : If the mount of Mercury is fully developed and the line of Mercury is straight, thin, deep and reddish, then that person has Bhadra Yog.

Result : A person born in Bhadra Yog is courageous, fearless and valiant. No enemy can ever stand before him. In a way, he is a great enemy-killer. He knows the art of making friendship with his enemies. His personality is quite influential. He always tries to achieve higher things. Whosoever comes in his contact, he is ready to help him. His brain is very sharp and fertile. So he can easily perform the most complex assignments also. Though such persons achieve height gradually but in the end, they reach the highest office. They have very favourable combination for business and they are able to spread their business abroad by their efforts.

Bhadra Yog

Note : This combination is due to the mount of Mercury. Mercury is the effective planet for business and wisdom. So it is certain that such a person is very wise and gains spectacular success in business. Such persons do not hesitate even in odd situations and find out their way among obstacles. They have a special power of taking decisions.

According to palmisty, the Ruchak Yog, Bhadra Yog, Hans Yog, Malavya Yog and Shash Yog are known as Panch-Maha Purush Yog.

Brahmacharya Yog or Combination of a Celebate

Definition : If there is a cross mark on the first phalange of the little finger, then there is the Brahmacharya Yog.

Result : One who has this combination remains away from women for the whole of his life. He does not get married and spends an ascetic's life.

Brahmacharya Yog

Santanheen Yog or Combination of Having No Offsprings

Definition : If there is a mark of star on the health line, then it is known as Santanheen Yog. Similarly, if there is a mark of star on the third phalange of the finger of Saturn, it is also called as Santanheen Yog.

Result : One who has this combination is devoid of the happiness of an issue. If this combination is found in the hands of both the husband and the wife, then surely, the couple will not have any issue.

Santanheen Yog

Vivah Yog or The Marriage Combination

Definition : If the mount of Venus and the mount of Jupiter are developed, then it combines to make Vivah Yog. But the learned palmists have specified the following facts for this yog as well such as:

Happy Marriage:

The origin of the fate line is the mount of Moon.

The fate line ends at the heart line.

There is a cross on the mount of Jupiter.

Vivah Yog

Unhappy Marriage:

The mount of Venus is under-developed.

There is red mark of star on the mount of Venus.

There is a mark of island on the marriage line.

There is a cross on the fate line.

Unequal Marriage:

The Sun line and the marriage line cut each other.

The mount of Venus is much more developed.

Marriage with a Trader:

A line goes to the mount of Venus from the bracelet.

A line goes to the mount of Mercury.

The Sun line has contact with the line of Venus.

Marriage with an Old Man:

A line goes to the mount of Venus or Saturn from the bracelet.

The hand is weak and narrow and the fate line and the marriage line are faulty.

Obstacles in Marriage:

The marriage line is broken at many places.

The mount of Moon has vertical and horizontal lines.

The mount of Venus has two star marks.

Divorce:

A line goes to the heart line from the line of Venus.

There is an island on the fate line.

There is a cluster of lines at the end of the marriage line.

Kleev Yog

Kleev Yog

Definition : The mount of Venus is suppressed more than necessary.

The palm has a ‘girdle of Venus' and the line of Mercury has contact with the line of Saturn.

There is a triangle in the middle of the palm and it has a point on it.

There is a yellow-coloured star mark on the mount of Venus.

The mount of Moon is very weak and has a mark of a triangle.

There is contact between the line of Saturn and the line of Moon and the fingers are of medium size.

Result : A person born with Kleev Yog is coward, weak and impotent. He can't satisfy his wife in the sexual act and his wife always dominates him. Such a man keeps away from risky tasks and hesitates while doing any new work. Such a man does not believe in quarrels and spends his life under the dominance of others.

Note : If there is the best combination of Raj Yog or one of the Pancha Maha Purush Yog and also the Kleev Yog, then the Kleev Yog destroys the good characteristics of all the good combinations. Such a man is compelled to live an ordinary life.

Dattak Putra Yog or Combination of Adoption

Definition : If the line of Saturn has contact with the line of Mars and both these mounts are fully developed, then there is Dattak Putra Yog.

Result : A person having this combination is adopted by other person either due to greed or due to being cared for or due to any other reason. Neither his real father nor he gets any benefit from each other.

Dattak Putra Yog

Note : To adopt a child or to give one's child in adoption to others is prevalent in the Indian society. Being not bestowed with a son or on the expiry of one's son, one adopts a son from his relatives and thus the adopted son becomes a full authority of the wealth and property of the adopter.

Matrityakta Yog

Definition : If the lines of Mars, Sun and Moon join with each other and the mount of Sun is weak and depressed, it is Matrityakta Yog.

Result : The person who has this yog, is abandoned by his mother after his birth due to some reasons and the child leads the life of an orphan.

Matrityakta Yog

Note : In Indian society, many times if a maiden gives birth to a child or a widow gives birth to a child, then the mother leaves the child for good due to her possible bad name in the society. Sometimes, due to poverty or due to some unavoidable circumstances, a child is relinquished. Such a child grows without his mother.

Matri Maran Yog or Combination of Mother's Death

Definition : If the line of Mars proceeding ahead divides the mount of Moon and the Moon line in two parts, then the person has Matri Maran Yog.

Result : In this combination, the mother of a child dies, sometime after the birth of the child.

Matri Maran Yog

Padjattva Prada Yog

Definition : If the Dragon's head and the mount of Moon are developed and the line of Moon has come into contact with the line of Dragon's head, then there is Padjattva Prada Yog.

Result : One who has this yog is born from the womb of his mother in the reverse way. He comes out feetways and not headways.

Padjattva Prada Yog

Anuddhapatyatva Sadhak Yog

Definition : If the mount of Venus and the mount of Moon are developed and a line goes from the mount of Venus to meet the line of Moon and the Sun line is weak, then there is Anuddhapatyatva Sadhak Yog.

Result : One who has such a combination is the child of a maiden or an unmarried woman.

Anuddhapatyatva Sadhak Yog

Note : If this combination is found in a man's palm, then he is not the issue of his father but of some other person or that child has been born from the womb of a maiden and has been relinquished. In the same way, if this combination is found in the palm of a woman, then she conceives it through other man and not through her husband or she conceives while she is a maiden.

Banchana-Chor-Meti-Yog

Definition : If the lines of Dragon's head, Saturn and Mars have joined and all these three mounts are fully developed, also the mounts of Jupiter and Sun are away from their respective places or are depressed, then this Yog is there.

Result : A person having this combination suffers from inferiority complex. He can be cheated by anyone easily and he comes in contact with such cheats in his life so many times that he loses everything.

Chor-Meti Yog

Note : A person having this combination is doubtful by nature. He always remains fearful and doubtful that he may not be cheated. Such a person can never do any work openly nor does he has the faith on anyone, nor can he share his innermost feelings with others. Such a person can never develop in his life.

Raj Laxmi Yog

Definition : If the mounts of Juipter, Venus, Mercury and Moon are fully developed and reddish, then the person has Raj Laxmi Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, progresses in his life by his own efforts. All his physical desires are fulfilled from time to time. He enjoys the happiness of vehicle, a house and wife. His personality in itself is greatly enamouring and attractive.

Raj Laxmi Yog

Note : The mounts of Moon and Mercury are able to give attractive and debonair personality to a person. So if the mounts of these two planets are fully developed, then surely the person is attractive and able. If both these planets are weak, then his personality and attraction are certainly not worth mentioning.

Gurukritorisht Bhang Yog

Definition : Jupiter is very important in the life of a person. So, if the mount of Jupiter is fully developed, the index finger has enough length and is a little inclined towards the finger of Saturn and the Juipter has a cross mark, then the above yog is formed.

Result : If the other lines and mounts are weak and undeveloped, they bring obstacles in life, but if the above combination is there in the palm, then all the evils are destroyed through it.

Gurukritorisht Bhang Yog

Rahukritorisht Bhang Yog

Definition : If the mount of the Dragon's tail is developed in the palm and the mount of Moon or any other mounts are weak due to the line of Dragon's tail with the sign of a triangle, then it is the above said yog.

Result : One who has this yog in his hand, is considered very powerful and an enemy-killer.

Rahukritorisht Bhang Yog

Ashubhkritorisht Bhang Yog

Definition : If the line of fate, the Sun line and the Moon line are very strong, then the above yog is possible.
