Result : Those who have this yog, their evils subside by themselves and these persons rise by their own efforts and also by the efforts of their friends.

Ashubhkritorisht Bhang Yog

Note : This combination means that if the mount and the lines of Saturn, Dragon's head, Dragon's tail and Mars are weak or broken or undeveloped, then all the evils connected with them are destroyed with the above combination.

Shubhkritorisht Bhang Yog

Definition : If anyone of the mounts of Juipter, Venus and Mercury and its corresponding lines are strong, prominent and clear, then this yog is formed.

Result : If there is any evil due to the auspicious planets, then it is rectified because of the above yog.

Shubhkritorisht Bhang Yog

Note : If any of the mounts related to auspicious planets is suppressed or any of the lines related to the auspicious planets is broken or weak but when the above combination is there in the palm, then the weakness of the auspicious planet is mitigated by it.

Kala Yog or Combination of an Artist

Definition : If any straight line coming from head line reaches the roots of the finger of Apollo or if the Sun line in both the hands starts from the life line, then the above combination is possible.

Result : One who has this combination earns his livelihood through the means of arts and he gains success in it.

Kala Yog

Note : If there is the above-mentioned combination in the palm of a person and his fingers are fat at their top, then he is not successful in arts. Similarly, if there are vertical and horizontal lines on the mount of Sun, then also he does not get success in the field of arts.

Vyapar Yog or Combination of a Trader

Definition : If the top of the finger of Apollo is square and the mount of Mercury is developed, then it is the Vyapar Yog.

Result : One who has this combination becomes a very successful businessman.

Vyapar Yog

Rasayan Shastra Yog or Chemist's Combination

Definition : If there are many vertical lines on the field of Mercury, then this combination is there.

Result : A person having this yog becomes famous in chemistry and is considered a learned man of Chemistry.

Rasayan Shastra Yog

Dharmik Yog or Religious Combination

Definition : If any line starting from the bracelets reaches the mount of Jupiter and the tops of the fingers are pointed, then it is called as Dharmik Yog.

Result : One who has this yog, reaches heights in the religious field. He writes religious books, becomes famous and earns honour.

Dharmik Yog

Antardrishti Yog or Combination of Insight

Definition : If the head line is narrow and long and reaches the mount of Moon, then it is the above yog.

Result : One who has this combination, can read the heart of others and can know about their feelings.

Antardrishti Yog

Rajnitigya Yog or Combination of Politicians

Definition : If the top of the finger of Saturn is pointed and the Sun line is developed and long, then this yog is there. If there is a triangle on the mount of Mercury, then also this yog is possible.

Result : One who has this yog, makes great progress in politics and earns respect in that field.

Rajnitigya Yog

Anveshan Yog or Inventor's Combination

Definition : If there is a white mark on the head line, then this yog is there.

Result : A person having this combination makes researches and invents new things and gets success.

Anveshan Yog

Note : If the mounts of Sun and Mercury are developed, then also this combination is possible. Also, if both the thumbs are inclined back or if the mount of Mercury is outside the palm, then also this combination and its results are possible.

Kanoon Yog or Lawyer's Combination

Definition : If the line of Saturn and the line of Jupiter are developed or if any line starts from the wrist line and reaches the mount of Jupiter, then there is the above yog.

Result : One who has this yog is a successful lawyer or a good judge and knows law thoroughly well.

Kanoon Yog

Chikitsak Yog or Physician's Combination

Definition : If the mount of Mercury is developed in both the hands and they have three vertical lines on them, then it is the Chikitsak yog or a doctor's combination.

Result : One who has this combination, is an expert physician or a doctor.

Chikitsak Yog

Sainik Yog or a Soldier's Combination

Definition : If there is a triangle on the mount of Mars in both the hands, then this yog is there.

Result : One who has this combination gets a high post in the armed forces.

Sainik Yog

Sahityik Yog or Combination of a Man-of-Letters

Definition : If the mount of Jupiter, the mount of Moon and the mount of Sun are developed and the Moon line goes upto the mount of Mercury, then there is the above yog.

Result : One who has this combination, becomes a successful man-of-letters.

Sahityik Yog

Note : Learned palmists have prescribed the following combinations for the above yog of man-of-letters:

There is a cross mark on the top of the index finger.

There is a mark of star on the mount of Mercury.

A staight line goes to the mount of the Sun from the bracelets.

There are white spots below the mount of Sun.

There are three or two and a half bracelets.

Bhagya Yog or Combination of Luck

Definition : When the fate line reaches the prominent mount of Sun, then there is the Bhagya Yog.

Result : A person having this combination is considered very lucky.

Note : The palmists have also considered the following combinations as Bhagya Yog :

Bhagya Yog

The fate line starts from the mount of Jupiter.

The fate line starting from the mount of Saturn, ends below the mount of Jupiter and there are white spots there.

The fate line is prominent and there is mark of a star on the mount of Sun.

Both the hands have clear and long fate lines.

There is a cross mark on the bracelets and the fate line is formed straight from there.

There is mark of a star on the mount of Jupiter.

The fate line starts from the mount of Moon.

Any line starting from the root of the finger of Apollo reaches upwards to the top.

The first line of bracelets is not chained and broken.

There is a triangle below the Sun line.

The heart line and the head line join below the mount of Jupiter.

A line goes from the mount of Venus to the mount of Mercury.

There is a vertical straight line on the mount of Jupiter.

There is a straight and clear line on the mount of Mercury.

Both the hands have developed mounts of Jupiter and the Sun lines are deep.

Bhagyoday Yog or Combination for Rise of Fate

Definition : If the fate line starts from the wrist line and goes upto the second phalange of the finger of Saturn, then it is the above-mentioned yog.

Result : One who has this combination, has the rise of his fate from the beginning of his life and he gets success in all walks of life due to his luck.

Bhagyoday Yog

Poorna Ayu or Combination for Full Age

Definition : If the life line is fully developed and goes upto the bracelets from its origin and there is no cross, point, spot or line over it, then he enjoys a full age.

Result : The person having the above combination enjoys full age with good health.

Note : Some experts have also called the following combinations as Poorna Ayu Yog:

Poorna Ayu Yog

There are three complete bracelets and the first bracelet is chained.

The life line touches the bracelet.

The fate line and the life line have mutual relationship.

The Sun line is faultless and is ahead of the head line.

All the mounts of the hands are fully developed and the thumb is long, thin, steadfast and inclined towards the back.

The health line is of full length and it has no cross or point.

Shatadhik Ayuryog or Combination for Living More Than 100 Years

Definition : If in both the hands of a person, the life line from its origin reaches the bracelets (after giving full vastness to the mount of Venus), then this yog is possible.

Shatadhik Yog

Result : One who has this combination, lives for more than a hundred years and his life is full of happiness.

Amit Mayu Yog

Definition : If the mounts of Mercury, Moon, Jupiter and Sun are developed in both the hands and the life line is faultless, long and clear, then the person has Amit Mayu Yog.

Result : One who has this yog becomes world-famous by his works. In a way, he never lacks anything in his life. His family life is full of happiness and economically, he is quite well-off and spends a happy, healthy and contented life for more than a hundred years.

Amit Mayu Yog

Note : This combination is considered the best in palmistry. A person with this combination becomes rich. He gets full happiness materially.

Mahabhagya Yog or Combination of Great Rise of Fate

Definition : If a person is born during day time, the Sun line is complete in length, the mount of Sun is developed and prominent at its place and the mounts of Moon and Jupiter are strong, then the person has Mahabhagya Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, has best ideas and leadership qualities in the society. Whosoever comes in contact with such a person, thinks himself quite lucky. Economically, such a person lacks nothing. Friends always co-operate with such a person. He spends his old age happily. Such a person gets success in life due to his own efforts. If a woman has this combination, then she gets married with a very high status person. Such a woman is well-behaved and gets respect in the society.

Mahabhagya Yog

Moksha Prapti Yog or Combination for Salvation

Definition : If the mount of Jupiter is developed and the line of Jupiter starting from Jupiter goes to the mount of Sun, then it is Moksha Prapti Yog.

Result : A person with this combination gets salvation after his death.

Moksha Prapti Yog

Note : According to the Hindu holy script, getting salvation is the best achievement. Only that man gets it who has led a virtuous, religious and benevolent life. He should also have the godly benediction. One who gets salvation, absolves himself from the cycle of birth and death forever. He devotes the whole of his life at the feet of God.

Such a person has full faith on God. He works justly. He is a great devotee, virtuous, benign, truthful and belongs to a good family.

Aswabhavik Mrityu Yog or Unnatural Death Combination

Definition : If a person has marks of cross on the life line on both his hands, then the person has unnatural death.

Result : One who has the above combination, dies an unnatural death.

Aswabhavik Mrityu Yog

Note : Palmists have mentioned the following combinations also as indicating unnatural death:

The life line is broken abruptly.

There is mark of a star at the beginning of the life line.

The life line is hair-like thin and unclear.

The life line is yellowish in colour.

There is a spot on the life line.

The beginning of the life line is clustered.

There are two lines at the beginning of the life line.

There is a mark of triangle on the life line.

The life line has entered the region of Venus.

The life line is very deep and wide in the palm.

The life line is quite small in the palm.

The life line, starting from its origin, has reached the second section of the wrist.

There is a triangle on the mount of Moon.

There are more than one spots on the mount of Moon.

There is a triangle on the Moon line.

The line of Moon, moving ahead cuts the life line and reaches the mount of Venus.

There is a mark of star on the third section of the finger of Apollo.

The health line is broken at many places.

There is a cross on the mount of Mercury.

The life line and health line are chained.

There are two triangles on the health line.

The above twenty-one combinations are also called the combinations of unnatural death. By unnatural death, I mean the following things :

Meeting death due to starvation and thirst in a jungle where one has lost one's way.

Being trampled under the feet of animals.

By drowning.

By the prick of thorns.

Quarrel leading to a fight.

Being in jail.

By a contagious disease.

Being crushed by a falling house.

Losing one's way in a forest.

By the fall of a tree.

With a rope.

Being poisoned by one's wife.

By eating rotten or diseased grain.

By wound becoming septic.

Being crushed by sticks and logs.

Being entangled in a family feud.

Being bound.

Any other reason leading to unnatural death.

Sarp Dansh Yog or Snake Bite Combination

Definition : If there is a girdle of Venus and it contains a triangle, then this combination is called Sarp Dansh Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, dies due to the bite of a snake.

Sarp Dansh Yog

Durmaran Yog or Combination of Unnatural Death

Definition : If there is a triangle on the region of the Dragon's tail and the mount of Sun is undeveloped, then it is known as Durmaran Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, does not die a natural death. He dies an unnatural death.

Note : Learned palmists have also indicated the following combinations to be of the above yog:

Durmaran Yog

There is a big triangle on the mount of Moon and another triangle inset in it.

There is a white mark on the life line.

The line of Dragon's head cuts the life line.

There is a mark of star on the Dragon's tail.

The life line is very small and there is cross mark at its end.

There are many triangles, spots and points in the whole of the hand.

The above combinations also indicate unnatural death. The following may be the reasons of such deaths:

Death with a weapon.

Death due to hanging.

Death due to eating poison.

Death due to burning.

Death due to injury in stomach with a weapon.

Violent blow on the navel causing death.

Kshay Rog Yog or Combination of Tuberculosis

Definition : If there is a circle on the mount of Moon and the Moon line cuts this circle, then there is this yog.

Result : Persons having this combination die due to Tuberculosis.

Kshay Rog Yog

Angaheen Yog or Combination of Limblessness

Definition : If there are two or more circles on the mount of Saturn and there is a relationship between the line of Saturn and the line of Mars, then it is the Angaheen Yog.

Result : A person having this combination has one of his organs cut in his life time.

Angaheen Yog

Kubadang Yog or Combination of a Honchback

Definition : If the lines of Saturn, Moon and the Dragon's head join together to make a triangle, then it is Kubadang Yog.

Result : One who is born in this yog, his back protrudes out and his chest goes in.

Kubadang Yog

Ekpad Yog or Combination of a Lame

Definition : It is found in a person whose health line has a triangle and from the triangle, a line goes to the mount of Moon.

Result : A person born with this combination is lame by one leg.

Ekpad Yog

Jad Yog or Combination of a Deaf

Definition : If there are many spots, points or horizontal and vertical lines on the mount of Moon, then it is called Jad Yog.

Result : A person born with this combination is deaf.

Jad Yog

Netra Nash Yog or Combination of Eye Trouble

Definition : If the line of the Dragon's head has contact with the Moon line, then there is the Netra Nash Yog.

Result : One who has this Yog, has weak eyes and he suffers from eye troubles.

Netra Nash Yog

Note : The following combinations are also considered Netra Nash Yog by learned palmists:

There is a mark of triangle on the mount of Sun.

The Sun line and the Moon line contact each other and make a cluster.

The Sun line is very weak.

The palm contains Moon line.

The mount of Sun has been displaced and has met the mount of Saturn.

The mount of Moon is increasing outside the palm.

There are two triangles on the Moon line.

The Sun line has reached the second phalange of the finger of Apollo.

There is a red spot in the centre of the palm.

Andh Yog or Blindness

Definition : If there are mounts of Mercury and Moon in the palm, then it is known as the Andh Yog.

Result : A person born with the above combination becomes blind.

Note : Palmists have also considered the following combinations as indicative of blindness:

Andh Yog

There is a cross mark on the Sun line.

The Sun line goes upto the mount of Mars and has a point at the end.

The Moon line turns and reaches the bracelet.

The line of Mars is moving outside the palm.

The line of Dragon's head has come into contact with the line of Mars.

There are red points on the Dragon's tail and the mount of Moon.

Shitla Yog or Smallpox Combination

Definition : If the lines of Sun, Saturn and Mars join below the mount of Juipter, then there is Shitla Yog.

Result : One who has Shitla Yog, suffers from smallpox in his life.

Shitla Yog

Sarp Bhay Yog (Fear from a Snake)

Definition : If the palm contains a chained line of Dragon's Head, then there is the above yog.

Resulta: A person having the above combination, is bitten by a snake. If the line is strong, then the person dies due to snake-bite.

Sarp Bhay Yog

Note : Palmists have described the following combinations as Sarp Bhay Yog also:

All the bracelets of the hand are chained.

There are signs of two triangles between the mounts of Dragon's Head and Dragon's Tail.

The health line is broken at many places and there is a black point at its end.

Grahan Yog

Definition : If the line of Dragon's Head and the line of Moon join firmly, then there is the Grahan Yog.

Result : A person having this combination remains disturbed with trouble throughout his life and faces obstacles in his life. He suffers from inferiority complex in his life.

Grahan Yog

Chandal Yog

Definition : If the lines of Jupiter and the Dragon's Head meet each other, then it is called as the Chandal Yog.

Result : A person born in this combination is unlucky. He struggles very hard for his livelihood. Such a person has retarded intelligence and is weak. He survives only through hard struggle.

Chandal Yog

Vrana Yog (Sepsis)

Definition : If there is a triangle on the line of Mars and in the middle of it is a white point, then there is the Vrana Yog.

Result : A person born with Vrana Yog dies due to septic of his wounds.

Vrana Yog

Galrog Yog or Combination of Throat Trouble

Definition : If the mount of Moon is suppressed and there is a net over its region, then it is Galrog Yog.

Result : A person born with this combination suffers from throat diseases throughout his life.

Galrog Yog

Lingsh Chhedan Yog or Crushing of Penis

Definition : If the line of Mercury has come into contact with the line of the Dragon's Head and there is a black sign at the point of their joining, then there is the above yog.

Result : A person having the above combination gets his penis crushed with a weighty article or he himself cuts it down.

Lingsh Chhedan Yog

Kalah Yog (Quarrels)

Definition : If the mount of Moon is protruded more than necessary in both the hands and there is a mark of a circle over it, then it is Kalah Yog.

Result : A person who has the above combination, spends the whole of his life in quarrels and he dies because of these quarrels.

Kalah Yog

Unmad Yog (Insanity)

Definition : If there is a mark of triangle on the mount of Sun and the Sun line cuts the triangle, then there is the Unmad Yog.

Result : A person having this combination talks irrelevantly.

Unmad Yog

Kushth Rog Yog or Combination of Leprosy

Definition : If the lines of Mars and Mercury join and go upto the bracelets, then this yog is there.

Result : One who has this combination, suffers from leprosy.

Kushth Rog Yog

Note : The following combinations also constitute the Kushth Rog Yog:

The Moon meets the Harshal.

There are two triangles in the palm and they cut each other.

Leprosy is a terrible disease. A person suffering from it has white spots on his body. The colour of his body changes and the wounds become septic.

Jalodar Rog Yog (Dropsy)

Definition : If the mount of Moon is well developed and the line of moon is full of ladders reaching the bracelet, then it is known as the Jalodar Rog Yog.

Result : A person having this combination suffers from Dropsy.

Jalodar Rog Yog

Note : A person suffering from Dropsy has much water collected in his stomach. His stomach continues to swell and meets his death due to it.

Muni Yog or Combination of a Monk

Definition : If the index finger is longer than the finger of Apollo, the mount of Juipter is protruded at its place and it has the benedictory mark (Swastik), then it is called as the Muni Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, keeps away from worldly affairs and spends the life of an ascetic remaining silent for most of the time.

Muni Yog

Note : If a person has the above combination, then he can neither remain social nor does he get the happiness of a family. He tends to become a hermit from his childhood and loves loneliness.

Kahal Yog

Definition : If the mount of Mars is developed and the lines coming out of it touch the mounts of Saturn, Mercury and Sun, then it is the Kahal Yog.

Result : A person born with this combination is physically strong and firm in character. He has great interest in doing courageous works. Such a person claims a high post in police or army. Materially, he lacks nothing but I have experienced it that such persons are not very wise. So once they reach the highest office, they soon decline. They do not get popularity corresponding to their post.

Kahal Yog

Budh Aditya Yog

Definition : This combination is possible if the mount of Mercury and the mount of Sun have intermingled with each other.

Result : A person having this combination is wise, clever and adopts himself according to the situation. Such a man becomes famous by his deeds, enjoys all the happiness and leads a very happy life.

Budh Aditya Yog

Diwaliya Yog or Combination of Insolvency

Definition : If the fate line is small and broken i at many places and there is a mark of an island on the health line, then there is the Diwaliya Yog in the palm of a person.

Result : A person having such a combination remains disturbed economically and in the end, he declares himself a bankrupt.

Diwaliya Yog

Jooa Yog or Combination of Gambling

Definition : If the finger of Saturn and the finger of Apollo are of equal length, then there is Jooa Yog.

Result : One who has this combination in his palm, earns wealth through gambling.

Jooa Yog

Lobh Yog or Combination of Greed

Definition : If the heart line moving straight goes across the palm, then it is called as the Lobh Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, is excessively greedy and miser.

Lobh Yog

Chori Yog (Theft)

Definition : If the mount of Mercury is developed and there is sign of a net on it, then it is Chori Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, faces thefts many times in his house.

Chori Yog

Note : Palmists also consider the following combinations as Chori Yog:

The mount of Mercury is quite developed and the health line has an island on it.

There is a point or cross on the last phalange of the little finger.

There are more than enough vertical lines on the third phalange of the finger of Apollo.

There is a cross mark on the last phalange of the little finger.

Chaap Yog

Definition : If the Sun line is composed of tiny lines, then it is called as the Chaap Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, has spent his childhood in great difficulty but after 28 years and onwards, he spends a fully contented life. Such a person loves travelling and collects money through travelling. Such a person is more proud than necessary.

Chaap Yog

Chhaap Yog

Definition : If the mount of Jupiter is developed and it has small lines forming a cross, then the person has Chhaap Yog.

Result : One who has the Chhaap Yog, rises to an extremely high post and spends life with happiness. Such a person can be called very lucky economically.

Chhaap Yog

Bheri Yog

Definition : If the mount of Jupiter in the right hand is developed and the line of Mercury has been formed (like a rope) by the combination of small lines but it is not broken, then it is called as Bheri Yog.

Result : A person born with such a combination is healthy, strong, wealthy, virtuous, clever, laborious and has longevity. He has a number of friends in his life and can make his enemies to be his friends.

Bheri Yog

Mridanga Yog

Definition : If the mount of Saturn is fully developed and the line of Saturn is composed of smaller lines, then it is called as the Mridanga Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, rises in his life on the basis of his luck and becomes famous due to his own efforts. He has his own way of working and so his work is always new and fine. Such a person wields great influence on others.

Bheri Yog

Srinath Yog

Definition : If the mount of Moon is developed and the Moon line is formed by the combination of small lines which proceed upwards and which are not broken, then it is known as the Srinath Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, he is economically wealthy, happy and prosperous. He lacks nothing from family life. His fortune always assists him and gains high level success due to his efforts.

Srinath Yog

Videsh Yatra Yog (Foreign Travels)

Definition : If the mount of Moon is developed in the palm and a straight line goes towards the mount of Mercury, then it is a foreign tour combination.

Result : A person having the above combination surely goes abroad.

Videsh Yatra Yog

Note : If any line issuing out of this line goes towards the mount of Sun, then the person being famous in a particular field goes abroad.

If any line coming out of it goes towards the mount of Saturn, then he goes abroad on a business trip.

If any line coming out of it goes towards the mount of Jupiter, then the person goes abroad to acquire education or on government assignment. If any assisting line coming out of it goes towards the mount of Venus, then the person goes abroad on a joy trip.

If any line issuing out of it goes towards the mount of Mars, then the person goes abroad on military errand or goes abroad because of his high post in army.

If any line coming out of it goes towards the mount of Harshal, then the person goes abroad for trade or to settle there permanently.

If any line coming out of it goes down, then his visit abroad is of a very short tenure and returns after infamy from there.

Besides, the following combinations have also been considered to suggest visits abroad:

(i) If any assisting line coming from the mount of Moon goes towards the mount of Venus and both the mounts of Venus and Moon are fully developed.

(ii) There is a mark of whirlpool on the mount of Moon.

(iii) There is a ring of Mercury on the mount of Mercury and a line originating from it goes towards the mount of Moon.

Pushkal Yog

Definition : If the mounts of Saturn and Venus are very prominently developed and reddish in colour and the fate line starts from the mount of Venus reaching the centre of the mount of Saturn, then the person has Pushkal Yog.

Result : A person having this combination is very handsome and attractive. His personality has impact on others very soon. Once he comes in contact with a person, he helps him in his happy days as well as in his days of misery and ties continues to pull on with it for the whole life. Economically, he lacks nothing. He likes to lead a very happy life. Such a person rises in his service due to his own efforts and gets success.

Pushkal Yog

Note : Learned palmists have considered the following combinations as Pushkal Yog also:

The fate line is straight, thin and clear and has connection with the mount of Moon, i.e., it has originated at the mount of Moon.

The fate line has originated from the mount of Mercury and reaches the mount of Saturn without breaking.

The fate line and the Moon line, both of them jointly reach the mount of Saturn.

The fate line starting from the first bracelet rises up and one of its subsidiaries reaches the mount of Sun.

Chamar Yog

Definition : If the fingers of the hand are long and the nails on them are reddish in colour; also the Sun line is long and prominent and has its origin from the bracelet with both the lines join at the place of their origin, then it is called as the Chamar Yog.

Result : A person born with this combination is highly respected and is worshipped by learned people. He is a learned person himself and becomes famous at home and abroad due to his wisdom and learning.

Chamar Yog

Such a person gets success due to his labour and earns fame on the strength of his success.

Note : A person born with this combination has longevity. By longevity, I mean age between seventy and a hundred years.

Malika Yog

Definition : Except the mounts of the Dragon's head and Dragon's tail, if all the other mounts of other planets are developed and prominent, then it known as the Malika Yog.

Result : If a person has Malika Yog, then he gets a high post in the government and gets respect and honour in the society for his leadership.

Malika Yog

Here, by seven planets, one means the planets of the Sun, the Moon, the Mars, the Mercury, the Jupiter, the Venus and the Saturn. It is to be noted that every mount has a central point and if the corresponding line of the planet touches clearly that central point of the mount, then that planet is the strongest one. Out of these seven planets, the Malika Yog will correspond to that one which is the strongest planet. To illustrate, if on the central point of the mount of Jupiter, the line of Jupiter touches, then the mount of Jupiter will be the strongest planet and hence, it will be termed as Jupiter Malika

Yog. Similarly, there can be Sun Malika Yog, Moon Malika Yog, etc. The results corresponding to these are as follows:

1. The Sun Malika Yog:

If the combination is due to Sun, then the person gets an important post in administration and reaches the post of a secretary by his efforts.

2. The Moon Malika Yog:

One who has this combination, becomes a commander in the navy or gets benefit by trading in cities near water. Such a person travels abroad many times.

3. Bhoam Malika Yog (Mars):

If the Malika Yog is corresponding to the planet of Mars, then the person gets high post either in army or in police. He gets the pleasures of wealth and amenities of a vehicle.

4. Mercury Malika Yog:

If the palm of a person has Mercury Malika Yog, then such a person is generous, philanthropist and benevolent. He goes abroad many times and earns fame and honour by his efforts.

5. Jupiter Malika Yog:

If there is Jupiter Malika Yog in the palm, then the person takes interest in holy scriptures and is a philanthropist and benevolent. Such a person always takes initiative in social works and gets full respect in the society.

6. Shukra Malika Yog (Venus):

One who has Shukra Malika Yog, is truly a devotee of his father and has no worry for wealth. Such a person is attractive and handsome and gets fame and respect by his works.

7. Shani Malika Yog (Saturn):

One who has Shani Malika Yog, has longevity but he has to struggle more than enough. He gets full fame and respect after the thirty-sixth year of his age.

Shankh Yog

Definition : If the area of the mount of Venus is vast and from it one line goes to the mount of Saturn and second goes to the mount of Sun, then it is called as Shankh Yog.

Result : One who has Shankh Yog, spends the whole of his life with happiness. His behaviour with others is simple and gentle and his wife is beautiful, congenial and educated. Such a person takes interest in religion and science. His life is a happy combination of materialism and spiritualism.

Shankh Yog

Veer Yog (Brave)

Definition : If the mount of Mars is prominent and developed and it has a mark of a circle, then it is Veer Yog.

Result : A person born with this combination takes lead in military or defence matters and reaches a very high post.

Veer Yog

Preshya Yog

Definition : If there is no fate line in the palm, then it is Preshya Yog.

Result : A person born with Preshya Yog is poor, unhappy and uneducated. He hears harsh words from others and works in slavery throughout his life.

Preshya Yog

Note : The following combinations are also called Preshya Yog by palmists :

The Sun line is broken at many places.

The Sun line originates either from the Dragon's Head or from the Dragon's Tail.

The fate line, along with the life line, goes out of the palm

Bhikshuk Yog (Beggar's Combination)

Definition : If there is a mark of cross on the fate line, then it is called Bhikshuk Yog.

Result : A person born with Bhikshuk combination is unlucky. He is deprived of the happiness of son, wife and family. He always remains in opposing circumstances. He always remains worried for his livelihood. He is indeed very poor.

Bhikshuk Yog

Note : Palmists have given the following combinations which also correspond to Bhikshuk Yog :

The mount of Venus is divided into two parts.

There is only one bracelet.

The mount of Mercury is outside the palm and contains white spots.

The Sun line has reached the second phalange of the finger of Apollo.

From the broken health line, a line issues and goes down to the bracelet.

Daridra Yog (Poverty)

Definition : If the Sun line is very weak and broken, then it is Daridra Yog.

Result : One who has Daridra Yog, is deprived of livelihood, is poor, anxious and remains continuously in misery.

Daridra Yog

Note : The following combinations also constitute Daridra Yog:

The mount of Venus contains a mark of a whirlpool or conchshell.

The finger of Saturn has a cross on the top.

There are many vertical and horizontal lines in the palm or there are nets.

Reka Yog

Definition : If the palm is shallow and there is a mark of a square in the centre of the palm, then it is called Reka Yog.

Result : One who is born with this combination is short of memory, vicious and is nearly a stupid fellow. He is always in hot waters. In a way, such a person is clever, back-biter, lazy and is conflicting. He is careless due to laziness and is unlucky.

Reka Yog

Note : Learned palmists have also considered the following combination of Reka Yog :

The mount of Venus and the mount of Jupiter are very weak and have white points on them.

There are more than two triangles in the palm.

There is a mark of cross on the mount of Mercury and a point below it.

The fingers and thumb of the hand are small and the nails thereon are yellowish, small and nearly round in form.

Raj Bhang Yog

Definition : The following combinations are considered as Raj Bhang Yog by the palmists :

Raj Bhang Yog

The joints of the fingers are swollen and are bulged out and increased.

All the fingers are flat and there are white spots on the index finger.

The front parts of the nails are flat and are inset.

There are three or four long lines on the first phalange of the thumb.

The front portions of the fingers are inclined towards the front.

The palm is quite thick and strong and the fingers are small.

All the fingers are long, strong and suppressed and have ugly joints.

There are two triangles on the mount of Moon and there is a point in between them.

The mount of Venus is outside the palm.

There are many thin lines originating from the line going downwards.

The head line is weak and has black spots.

The lines of the Sun and the Jupiter are wavy.

There is a net on the centre of the palm and a similar net on the mount of Sun.

There are marks of stars on all the first phalanges of the fingers.

The line of Jupiter is ladder-shaped.

There is a mark of an island below the Sun line.

The lines of Venus and Moon are full of spots.

The beginning of the Sun line is tasseled.

There is a net on the mount of Mercury and on its first phalange, there is a point mark.

There are zig-zag lines on the mount of Saturn. These cut each other.

There is a mark of Saturn on the mount of Moon.

The hand lacks the mounts of Sun and Mercury.

There is a sign of a circle on the mount of Mercury.

There is net or point or cross on the high Moon.

The life line is broken in between and it is thick and red in colour.

The head line is chained.

There are many branches in the beginning of the life line.

A line originating from the life line and tasseled in shape goes to the mount of Moon.

The heart line is broken at two or three places.

There is a sign of wheel between the Jupiter and the Saturn.

The head line is yellow and weak.

There is sign of cross between the heart line and the life line.

The head line is narrow and thin and goes out of the palm.

The life line is inclined towards the mount of Moon and there is a mark of a star on the index finger.

The head line is much broken and weak in both the hands.

36. There are white marks on the head line.

Result : There may be many good combinations in the palm but one who has Raj Bhang Yog, is full of misery, troubles, anxiety and leads a life full of poverty.

Raj Rajeshwar Yog

Definition : If the mount of Sun is developed and the Sun line, coming into the centre of the palm, goes towards the mount of Venus and there is no restriction on the line, then it is Raj Rajeshwar Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, leads a life of extreme happiness, wealth and prosperity and enjoys many luxuries of life.

Raj Rajeshwar Yog

Note : The following combinations are also related with it :

The palm is long and there are no gaps between each finger.

The fingers of Apollo and the little finger are equal.

Brahmand Yog

Definition : The hand is ideal and if it has relationship between the mount of Moon with the mount of Venus through two long lines, then it is Brahmand Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, is economically prosperous and leads a royal life.

Brahmand Yog

Note : Learned palmists have also considered the following combinations as Brahmand Yog :

There are three or four vertical lines on the second phalange of the finger of Saturn.

All the fingers have small half-moons below them.

The thumb is long, thin and inclined to the back and the mount of Venus is fully developed.

There are two lines coming out of the mount of Moon, one goes towards the mount of Venus and the second goes towards the mount of Mercury.

There is an assisting line going along the Sun line.

Laxmi Yog (Wealth Combination)

Definition : If there are the ring of Saturn and the ring of Mercury and one line joins both of these rings, then it is Laxmi Yog.

Result : A person having Laxmi Yog gets admiration from the society and is economically quite prosperous. Such a person has enough power to deliver speeches and he can turn people in his favour by it. Such a person is virtuous, clever and famous.

Laxmi Yog

Mahalaxmi Yog (Great Wealth Combination)

Definition : If the fate line is quite straight, clear, has full length, originates from the wrist line and it reaches the central point of the mount of Saturn, and the Sun line originating from the mount of Moon reaches the mount of Sun, then the palm has Mahalaxmi Yog.

Result : A person having Mahalaxmi Yog has uncountable wealth. He never lacks anything economically. He enjoys all the amenities of material prosperity to the fullest.

Mahalaxmi Yog

Bharati Yog

Definition : If the mount of Mercury and the mount of Jupiter are developed and the lines of Mercury and Jupiter are ladder-like, then it is Bharati Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination is handsome and attractive in personality. Such a person is himself an artist and benefits the artists by helping them. Such a person is virtuous, clever, learned and is famous in arts like music, etc.

Bharati Yog

Note : While noting the above combination and observing the mounts of Mercury and Jupiter, one should also observe that the fingers of the person are long, thin, without knots and beautiful.

Arvind Yog

Definition : If all the mounts in the palm are prominent and protruded and there is an assisting line going along the life line and also there is a mark of cross on the mount of Jupiter with the line of health and head strong, then it is Arvind Yog.

Result : A person having this combination leads the life of a king. He is honoured in the society and by his works, he provides leadership to the country and the society. He never lacks anything economically.

Arvind Yog

Note : Palmists have also considered the following combinations as Arvind Yog :

The fate line originates from the middle of the life line and goes to the mount of Saturn.

Two branches emanate from the life line and go to the mounts of Mercury and Sun.

The index finger is inclined towards the finger of Saturn and the index finger and the finger of Apollo are equal in length.

Tarit Yog

Definition : If a thin line originating from the mount of Moon goes towards the mount of Jupiter and the small finger is nearly equal to the finger of Apollo, then it is Tarit Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, leads the life of a king in his life. He gets full respect and honour in the society and is economically quite happy.

Tarit Yog

Note : Palmists have also considered the following combinations as that of Tarit Yog :

The fingers are thin and long and there is a black point on the third phalange of the index finger.

The Sun line is like a ladder and has established contact with the health line.

The finger of Apollo is inclined towards the little finger and all the mounts are developed.

Saraswati Yog or Combination of Wisdom

Definition : If a line originating from the mount of Jupiter reaches the mount of Moon and another line originating from the mount of Moon reaches the mount of Jupiter with both the mounts fully developed, then it is Saraswati Yog.

Result : A person having Saraswati Yog is very famous. He is considered favourite of Goddess Saraswati (of learning). He is expert in poetry, music and dance and in a particular art, he achieves excellence. He earns fame in his country and abroad due to his art. Such a person gains favour of Saraswati and Laxmi both. Such a person is sensitive and generous. He is always ready to serve the poor, the troubled and the poverty-striken.

Saraswati Yog

Kailash Yog

Definition : If the lines coming out of the mounts of Mercury and Sun join each other below the health line and a sign of triangle is thus formed, then it is Kailash Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, leads the life of a king. He gains full happiness materially and spiritually.

Kailash Yog

Note : Learned Palmists have considered the following combinations also as Kailash Yog :

There is a mark of fish on the first bracelet and one line goes from there to the mount of Moon.

There are two life lines.

There are two fate lines clearly visible.

Rashmi Yog

Definition : If a line starting from the bracelets reaches the mount of Venus and the mount of Venus is very prominent, protruded, beautiful, sober and reddish, then it is Rashmi Yog.

Result : One who is born with this combination, never lacks anything in his life and due to his labour, he is able to earn the happiness and pleasure of a vehicle, wealth, reputation and honour.

Rashmi Yog

Note : The following combinations have also been called Rashmi Yog by the learned palmists :

If there is the mark of ‘Swastik' (a benedictory mark), then it is Rashmi Yog.

There are one vertical and the other horizontal lines on the mount of Moon but there should not be the mark of a cross below it.

There are two health lines in the palm.

Divya Yog

Definition : In the right hand of a person if the line of Jupiter reaches the mount of Venus but the Sun line and the line of Mercury are strong in themselves, then it is Divya Yog.

Result : One who is born with the above combination, enjoys life fully. He collects wealth by much labour. He is honoured in the society. Economically, he is quite prosperous and happy.

Divya Yog

Note : Palmists have also considered the following combinations as Divya Yog :

A line starting from the mount of Mars goes to the mount of Sun and another line starting from the mount of the Sun goes to the mount of Mars.

The mount of Moon is prominent and strong and has relationship with the fate line.

The mount of Mercury and the mount of Moon have been joined by a line.

There are two head lines which do not meet each other.

Maharajadhiraj Yog

Definition : If the mounts of Sun, Moon, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus and Saturn are fully developed and all the lines connected with them are prominent, strong, thin and straight, then it is Maharajadhiraj Yog.

Result : A person born with this combination is considered very fortunate. He is considered very strong and firm economically and also in business. Such a man enjoys all the luxuries of life and also goes to pilgrimages many times. He offers charities and is always first in religious works. His life may be considered perfect from all aspects of life.

Maharajadhiraj Yog

Note : The following combination is also considered as Maharajadhiraj Yog :

1. The fate line is dual and is very thin. Along with it, the life line, the head line and the health line are also dual but these lines should not come in contact with each other.

Dewansh Yog

Definition : If out of the seven planets, five mounts are developed and are prominent and also their corresponding lines are clear and faultless, then it is Dewansh Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, leads a royal life. He enjoys all the comforts and luxuries of life.

Dewansh Yog

Parawat Yog

Definition : If out of the seven planets, four planets have developed, strong and prominent mounts and their corresponding lines are straight, faultless and simple, then it is Parawat Yog.

Result : One who has Parawat Yog in his palm, is economically very prosperous and lacks nothing in enjoying life.

Parawat Yog

Nrip Yog

Definition : If out of the seven planets, three planets have strong, prominent and reddish mounts and their corresponding lines are also clear, then it is Nrip Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, is economically prosperous and enjoys the happiness of having vehicle, land, house and family, etc. He enjoys all these.

Nrip Yog

Gauri Yog

Definition : If out of the seven planets in the palm, the mounts of two of them are prominent and strong and if their corresponding lines are clear, then there is Gauri Yog in the palm.

Result : One who has Gauri Yog, is a landlord and has interest in agricultural works. He looks after all the members of his family with great love and affection. Such a person is religious and likes to worship God. His sons are also able, virtuous and of high quality.

Gauri Yog

Raj Yog

Definition : If out of the seven planets of the palm, any one planet has a strong and reddish mount and if its corresponding line is also simple, straight and clear, then it is Raj Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, rises due to his labour and makes his life comfortable. If other lines are also favourable, then he becomes a Gazetted Officer. Such a person collects wealth by his hard labour and also enjoys it.

Raj Yog

Rajya Yog

Definition : One who has a strong Sun line and a very good mount of Jupiter, has Rajya Yog.

Result : One who has Rajya Yog, tries to lead a life full of comforts and luxuries. At the flag end of his career, nearly at the age of 42, his fate rises and he gets land, house and comfort of a vehicle. Such a person is clever and able. He keeps patience even in difficult times.

Rajya Yog

Note : The following combinations are also Rajya Yog :

The mount of Jupiter is very good and all the fingers are long and thin.

The little finger is unduly long.

There is a mark of ‘Swastik' (a benedictory mark) below the mount of Venus.

The mount of Jupiter is quite big and a line proceeding from there goes towards the mount of Sun.

The fate line originates from the bracelet.

The mount of Jupiter is not outside the palm and is in the best position in itself.

Mahendra Yog (King's combination)

Definition : If in both the hands the fate line and the Sun line originate from the bracelet and the mount of Moon is fully developed, then it is Mahendra Yog.

Result : One who has the above combination of the Mahendra Yog, is economically quite prosperous and leads the life of a king. All his desires are fulfilled in his life time.

Mahendra Yog

Note : The following combinations are also Mahendra Yog :

The mount of Moon is strong and it has a benedictory mark (Swastik) over it.

The mount of Venus is strong and has increased towards the life line and also has a benedictory mark (Swastik) over it.

Rudra Yog

Definition : If there are the rings of Juipter and that of Saturn in the palm and a line originating from the Sun touches these two rings, then it is Rudra Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, is quite a happy-go-lucky type of person. Though he earns enough wealth, but he also spends it. He believes in show, decorum and exhibition of his prosperity.

Rudra Yog

Mrigendra Yog

Definition : If the mounts of Sun, Moon, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus and Saturn are suppressed and their corresponding lines are weak and many lines are not even visible, then it is Mrigendra Yog.

Result : One who is born with the above combinations, spends a royal life and is quite high economically; lacks nothing whatsoever economically.

Mrigendra Yog

Dev Yog

Definition : If out of the seven planets, the mounts of six of the planets are suppressed and invisible and the lines corresponding to them are also invisible or broken, then it is Dev Yog.

Result : One who is born with the above combination, enjoys all the pleasures of life. His life is very peaceful and simple and he becomes famous in his particular field. This combination should be considered equivalent to Raj Yog.

Dev Yog

Vikram Yog

Definition : If out of the seven planets in the palm, the mounts of five of the planets are suppressed and invisible and their corresponding lines are also invisible and broken, then he has Vikram Yog.

Result : One who has this Yog, collects wealth by his labour and is able to lead an honourable life in the society. He lacks nothing economically.

Vikram Yog

Surpati Yog

Definition : If among the mounts of seven planets four mounts are suppressed, weak or invisible and lines related to these are also invisible or broken, then there is Surpati Yog.

Result : A person born with the Surpati Yog earns wealth due to his own efforts and gets respect in the society. Materially, his life is full of happiness.

Surpati Yog

Gajpati Yog

Definition : If out of the mount of seven planets, three of them are quite suppressed, weak or invisible and their corresponding lines are also invisible and broken, then it is Gajpati Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination is very laborious and always moves towards progress. He wants to do something specific in his life. His fortune rises after the 32nd year of his life and after that he enjoys all the pleasures of his life till the end of it.

Gajpati Yog

Man Mahendra Yog

Definition : If the mounts of Harshal, Neptune and Pluto are strong and if the mount of Dragon's Head is connected with the mount of Dragon's Tail by a line, then it is Man Mahendra Yog.

Result : A person born with the Man Mahendra combination is strong, virtuous and honourable. He becomes famous in a particular field. He gets a high administrative post and makes his life full of happiness.

Man Mahendra Yog

Harihar Brahm Yog

Definition : If in the right hand the mounts of Mercury, Moon, Venus and the Sun are joined with one another by lines and the lines do not cut one another at any point, then it is Harihar Brahm Yog.

Result : A person who has Harihar Brahm combination, leads a purely cultural life and is truthful. He knows the holy scriptures and worships God. Such a person enjoys special honour in the society.

Harihar Brahm Yog

Kusum Yog

Definition : If the mount of the Sun has mingled with the mount of the Saturn, then it is Kusum Yog.

Result : A person having Kusum Yog is considered learned and lucky. He gets a high post. Such a person gets some sudden moments when he is able to progress quickly. Such a person does not lack anything in his life.

Kusum Yog

Agnikand Yog

Definition : If there is a mark of cross on the mount of Mars and a line originating from this mount goes to the mount of Dragon's Head, then there is Agnikand Yog.

Result : One who has such a combination, dies in a fire accident.

Agnikand Yog

Matsya Yog or Fish Combination

Definition : If the mount of Venus is developed, the thumb is thin, long and slightly inclined back and along with it if the line of the Sun is ladder-shaped, then it is Matsya Yog.

Result : One who has Matsya Yog, is a correct and accurate fortune-teller, is punctual and is full of virtues. Such a man progresses by his wisdom. He is considered kind, wise, virtuous and forbearing.

Matsya Yog

Agraj Ghatak Yog

Definition : If there is sign of a triangle on the mounts of Harshal and Dragon's Head, then the incumbent has Agraj Ghatak Yog.

Result : One who has the above combination, keeps enmity with his elder brother or even kills him.

Agraj Ghatak Yog

Koorma Yog

Definition : If the Sun line passes clearly over the mount of Moon, joins the life line in its centre and the fate line is strong, then it is Koorma Yog.

Result : A person born with the Koorma Yog combination becomes famous and respectable by his works. Such a person remains patient in odd circumstances, is well-versed in delivering lectures and influencing others. He leads a royal life.

Koorma Yog

Atmaghat Yog or Suicide Combination

Definition : If there is a mark of star on the third phalange of the finger of Saturn, then it is Atmaghat Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, dies because of his own affairs and commits suicide.

Atmaghat Yog

Devendra Yog

Definition : One who has a sign of a lotus in one's palm near the life line but slightly away from the mount of Luna, but which does not cut any famous line, then it is Devendra Yog.

Result : One who has Devendra Yog, is a clever and a fore-sighted man. He has enigmatic personality and has the ability to influence others. Such a person is disciplined, handsome, able and adept. He has longevity.

Devendra Yog

Khang Yog

Definition : If the mount of Mercury has slipped from its original place and has mixed with the mount of Sun, and the mount of Sun is firm and prominent in itself, then it is Khang Yog.

Result : A person who has the above combination rises to a very high standard of life though he is born in very ordinary circumstances. Whatever such persons say, people try to adhere to them. Such persons are successful and best in fortune-telling. They do not lack anything economically.

Khang Yog

Navlaxmi Yog

Definition : If the fate line, the Sun line and the line of Mercury originate from the bracelets and are straight, simple and clear, then it is Navlaxmi Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, becomes the owner of uncountable wealth. He is prosperous, famous and majestic.

Navlaxmi Yog

Jyotirvid Yog or Astrologer's Combination

Definition : If there are no interfering lines on the mount of Venus and this mount is prominent in itself then it is Jyotirvid Yog.

Result : A person born with the above combination is a very successful fortune-teller.

Jyotirvid Yog

Bhoomi Yog

Definition : If the mount of Mars and the mount of Venus are connected by a line and an assisting line of the fate line meets on the mount of Venus, then it is Bhoomi Yog.

Result : One who has the above combination, gets special benefits from works related to land and is a landlord in himself.

Bhoomi Yog

Putratah Dhanapti Yog

Definition : If a thin line starting from the mount of Jupiter moves on to the mount of Venus and there is a black sign (Til) between the index finger and the finger of Saturn, then it is the above combination.

Result : One who has the above combination, gets money from his son for the whole of his life. He also gets the happiness from his encumbrances.

Putratah Dhanapti Yog

Koteesh Yog

Definition : If a person has a ring of Saturn and from this ring, smaller lines rise above, and if the fate line originates from the bracelets going upto the mount of Saturn or the finger of Saturn, then it is Koteesh Yog.

Result : A person having the Koteesh Yog is surely a multimillionaire in his life.

Koteesh Yog

Arishta Yog

Definition : If the mount of Saturn has the sign of a star and the first phalange of the finger of Saturn has a white mark, then it is Arishta Yog.

Result : A person having Arishta Yog suffers many times in his life from charms, ghosts, magics, spells, etc.

Arishta Yog

Kalah Yog

Definition : If there is a net of perpendicular and horizontal lines on the mount of Luna, then it is Kalah Yog.

Result : A person having Kalah Yog is quarrelsome with everyone throughout his life and has mental imbalance with everyone.

Kalah Yog

Unmad Yog

Definition : If the mount of Luna and the Dragon's Head are related by a line or the mount of Sun and the Dragon's Head are related with each other, then there is Unmad Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination is perplexed and always in a state of anxiety.

Unmad Yog

Vish Yog (Poisoning)

Definition : If there are many black spots below the line of the Mercury, then it is Vish Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination is killed by being poisoned fraudulently.

Vish Yog

Shwan Yog (Dog Bite)

Definition : If there are black points or spots on the mount of Saturn, then it is Shwan Yog.

Result : A person having this combination suffers from the bite of a mad dog and suffers from Hydrophobia and dies.

Shwan Yog

Vrihad Beej Yog

Definition : If there are black points on the spot on Dragon's Head, then it is Vrihad Beej Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination suffers throughout his life from enlarged testicles.

Vrihad Beej Yog

Vimal Yog

Definition : If there are white spots between two bracelets, then there is Vimal Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination is full of all virtues. He enjoys all the comforts and pleasures of life. He is kind, benevolent and a happy person.

Vimal Yog

Saral Yog

Definition : If there are small half-moons on all the nails of a person, then he has Saral Yog.

Result : A person having Saral Yog has longevity, education, understanding with insight and progress.

Saral Yog

Harsha Yog

Definition : If there is a black point (Til) in the centre of the palm (between the life line and the mount of Luna), then it is Harsha Yog.

Result : A person having Harsha Yog is virtuous, lucky, clever, firm and famous.

Harsha Yog

Pravrijya Yog

Definition : If there is a triangle on the life line, then it is Pravrijya Yog.

Result : One who has the above combination, becomes an ascetic and leaves domestic life after receiving initiation from a teacher monk.

Pravrijya Yog

Note : Learned palmists have considered the following combinations also as Pravrijya Yog :

The Sun line has a triangle.

The whole of the hand is flexible and all the fingers are long and beautiful and the fate line has a triangle on it.

The mount of Mercury has a triangle or a net below it.

There is net on the lines of encumbrances and the lines of love.

Shukra Yog

Definition : If the mount of Venus is strong, prominent, protruded and beautiful and it has no net of interfering lines, then it is Shukra Yog.

Result : A person having Shukra Yog is wealthy, learned, famous and respectable. Such a person has longevity and a happy family life.

Shukra Yog

Dur Yog

Definition : If there is a mark of triangle on the health line, then it is Dur Yog.

Result : A person having Dur Yog is physically weak, careless about his health, selfish and leads a poor life. He lives away from his birth place.

Dur Yog

Gol Yog

Definition : If the mount of Venus has come out of the palm, then it is Gol Yog.

Result : Such a person is laborious, unlucky, not intelligent enough and always runs after wealth.

Gol Yog

Yug Yog

Definition : If the health line goes out of the palm and has connection with the life line, then it is Yug Yog.

Result : A person having this combination is quick-tempered, proud and licentious. Such a person does not get any honour in the society.

Yug Yog

Shool Yog

Definition : If the line of Mars passing over the mount of Moon goes to the mount of Sun, it is Shool Yog.

Result : A person having the Shool Yog is wrathful, quarrelsome, proud and poor. He spoils his work due to his wrath.

Shool Yog

Kedar Yog

Definition : If the line of Sun is inclined towards the mount of Saturn and connects itself with the health line, then it is Kedar Yog.

Result: A person having this combination is lazy, sexy, helpful to his relatives and is adept in agriculture.

Kedar Yog

Note : This combination has a mixture of good and evil virtues in a person.

Paash Yog

Definition : If there is a black mark on the mount of Jupiter, then it is Paash Yog.

Result : A person having this combination is very popular among his friends, gets happiness through service and is wealthy.

Paash Yog

Damini Yog

Definition : If there is a black mark (Til) between the finger of Saturn and the finger of Apollo, then it is Damini Yog.

Result : One who has this combination, has a high moral character. He helps others and is wise.

Damini Yog

Mukut Yog

Definition : If the mount of Mars is developed in the right hand and a line passing from there goes to the mount of Sun with a benedictory mark, then it is Mukut Yog.

Result : A person having this combination is a very good player in his life time. He is recognised in the society as a player and becomes famous. Such a person has strong feeling of revenge. Such a person at once recognises his prey and also his aim.

Mukut Yog

Rin Yog (Debt)

Definition : If there is a black mark on the right side of the line of Saturn, then it is Rin Yog.

Result : A person having this combination remains in debt throughout his life. His income is always less compared to his expenditure.

Rin Yog

Karak Yog

Definition : If there is a red or black mark on the left side of the line of the Jupiter, then it is Karak Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination leads his life happily. He attains high standard in his business. He never faces any obstacles or hindrance materially or economically.

Karak Yog

Vallaki Yog

Definition : If there is a red mark below the line of Mars or at its right, then it is Vallaki Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination is peaceful, sober, cheerful and adept in his profession. He gets cooperation from many of his friends.

Vallaki Yog

Sharda Yog

Definition : If there is black mark below the mount of Mercury or at its left, then it is Sharda Yog.

Result : A person having this combination is bestowed with men, women and relatives and is progressive. He honours God. He is a learned, clever and a religious person.

Sharda Yog

Samudra Yog or Sea Combination

Definition : If there is a black mark below or at the right of the line of the Sun, then it is Samudra Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination is famous. He keeps ahead in religious affairs. He enjoys a princely life and has no worries.

Samudra Yog

Ardha-Chandra Yog or Half-Moon Combination

Definition : If there is a black point or red point on the left or right side of the Moon line, then it is Ardha-Chandra Yog.

Result : A person having this Yog remains happy and cheerful throughout his life. He is also prosperous, generous, attractive and progressive constantly. He lacks nothing in his life.

Ardha-Chandra Yog

Chhatra Yog

Definition : If there is a black or red point near or below the line of Venus, then it is Chhatra Yog.

Result : If a person has Chhatra Yog, then he is courageous, lover of things, enjoys full family life and spends his life joyfully.

Chhatra Yog

Koot Yog

Definition : If there are more than two black points in the palm and they come within the closed fist, then it is Koot Yog.

Result : Those persons who have Koot Yog are very courageous and continuously go on progressing. They are not afraid of odd circumstances but they struggle and make them according to their way.

Koot Yog

Ishu Yog

Definition : If there is a black point on the first phalange of the thumb, then it is Ishu Yog.

Result : A person born with the above combination is a gazetted officer. Such persons are mostly Jail Officers or Police Superintendents. They achieve their aims fully in their life time.

Ishu Yog

Digbal Yog

Definition : If there are black or red points below the mounts of Sun, Mercury or Jupiter or lines concerned with these planets (excluding the Saturn), then it is Digbal Yog.

Result : One who has the above combination, reaches a very high standard and is respected by the society for his works.

Digbal Yog

Neech Bhang Raj Yog

Definition : If there is a girdle of Venus in the palm and it has a cross mark, then it is Neech Bhang Raj Yog.

Result : A person who has this combination makes progress by his own efforts and gets happiness in the economic, social and political fields.

Note : Learned palmists have termed the following combinations as also pertaining to Neech Bhang Raj Yog.

Neech Bhang Yog

There is a benedictory sign in the centre of the palm.

All the three phalanges of the finger of Saturn have black points (Tils).

There is the ring of Jupiter and there is cross mark on the mount of Jupiter.

There is the ring of Mercury but a line starting from the mount of Luna reaches the mount of Mercury.

All the mounts of the palm are generally prominent and good.

The fate line is laddered.

The life line is full and has no obstructing lines.

The mount of Venus is protruded and has no obstructing lines.

There are four bracelets and the first one is chained.

Chandika Yog

Definition : If the palm is longer and not wider, the fingers are not knotty and a line, originating from the starting point of the life line, goes to the mount of the Sun or the Mercury, then it is Chandika Yog.

Result : A person having Chandika Yog has moral character and wealth. He always looks to the welfare of others and progresses continuously. He leads the life of a minister.

Chandika Yog

Nabhas Yog

Definition : If there is a benedictory mark below any finger or on any mount, then it is Nabhas Yog.

Result : A person who has this combination is honourable, respectable, benevolent and enjoys full material happiness. Such persons surely get complete success in their lives.

Nabhas Yog

Jai Yog

Definition : A person who has two or more than two black marks on the mount of Mars, has Jai Yog.

Result : A person who has this combination, is strong and fortunate. He gets victory in all his works. He destroys his enemies quickly. He is able, virtuous, patient and lives long.

Jai Yog

Vidyat Yog

Definition : If there is a black mark above the bracelets, then it is Vidyat Yog.

Result : A person having such a combination leads a royal life, is wealthy and enjoys full happiness.

Vidyat Yog

Shiv Yog

Definition : If there is a wavy line in the centre of the palm, then it is Shiv Yog.

Result : A person having Shiv Yog is a high class businessman or gets success in achieving a high post in the army. Such a person becomes a police officer or leads a contented life.

Shiv Yog

Vishnu Yog

Definition : If there is a lotus-like mark on the palm, then it is Vishnu Yog.

Result : A person having Vishnu Yog owns a great pomp and is very prosperous, greatly fortunate and leads a very happy life. He influences others by his lectures and spends a very contented life.

Vishnu Yog

Brahm Yog

Definition : Learned palmists believe that a person having the sign of a flag in his palm has Brahm Yog.

Result : A person born with Brahm Yog leads a prosperous life. Such a person has full faith on God, brahmins and monks. He is also a man of character and engages himself for the welfare of the society.

Brahm Yog

Hari Yog

Definition : A person having the sign of a house in his palm is bestowed with Hari Yog.

Result : A person bestowed with Hari Yog leads a life of pomp and glory. He is learned, clever and adept in conversation. He influences others with his own personality. He lacks nothing economically.

Hari Yog

Har Yog

Definition : If there is a sign of trident but it does not cut the life line, then it is Har Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination progresses continuously, takes interest in religious works, is benevolent, kind, truthful and helps others.

Har Yog

Brahma Yog

Definition : If a person has the mark of an elephant on the mount of Venus near the bracelets, then he has Brahma Yog.

Result : A person having Brahma Yog is sharp and intelligent. Such a person is calm, patient, sober and a social reformer. He has enough confidence on himself.

Brahma Yog

Ravi Yog

Definition : A person having a mark of the Sun has the Ravi Yog.

Result : A person having this combination takes interest in scientific research and is very intelligent. He follows high thinking and simple living. He has an ordinary personality but his fame spreads far and wide. He earns honour and respect in his life.

Ravi Yog

Pati Tyag Yog

Definition : A woman who has the mark of a circle on the mount of Jupiter suffers from the separation of her husband.

Result : A woman who has such a combination is either divorced or left by her husband.

Pati Tyag Yog

Note : If a man has such a sign, then the woman leaves him or she divorces him.

Garbhpat Yog (Abortion)

Definition : One who has the mark of a hill made by lines on the mount of Venus, then it is Garbhpat Yog.

Result : A woman who has the above combination gets abortion due to certain reasons.

Garbhpat Yog

Note : There are following more combinations in this regard :

There are white points on the lines of encumbrances.

There is a horizontal line on the lines of the encumbrances.

Yup Yog

Definition : If there is sign of a tree anywhere on the palm, then it is Yup Yog.

Result : A person who has the above combination is self-centred, selfish, contented and is weak in character.

Yup Yog

Nav Yog

Definition : If there is mark of a column anywhere on the palm, then it is Nav Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination is happy, successful and fully satisfied in domestic life.

Nav Yog

Dand Yog

Definition : If there is the sign of a sword in anybody's palm, then he has Dand Yog.

Result : One who has the above combination, has always differences with his wife. He does not get satisfaction of the encumbrances. Thus, in a way he lacks something in his domestic life.

Dand Yog

Shakti Yog

Definition : If there is a mark of a bow in anybody's palm, then he has Shakti Yog.

Result : A person having the Shakti Yog is selfish, greedy, lazy and is insulted by others. If at all he has money, he spends it foolishly. Such a person is not honoured by the society.

Shakti Yog

Shri Mahalaxmi Yog

Definition : If there is sign of scales anywhere on the palm of a person, then it is Shri Mahalaxmi Yog.

Result : One who has such a combination, has lakhs of rupees and is religious-minded. His fame spreads far and wide. He is happy, prosperous and contented in all walks of life.

Shri Mahalaxmi Yog

Definition : If there is sign of a metal pot on the palm, then it is Dhan Vriddhi Yog.

Result : One who has the above combination, gets wealth continuously and his bank balance always increases.

Result : One who has the above combination, gets wealth suddenly many times in his life.

Result : One who has this combination, is benevolent, truthful and religious. He has faith in God.

Result : A person having the above combination is wrathful, cruel, notorious and dare-devil. Such a combination can be found in the hands of dacoits.

Definition : A person having the mark of a chariot has Nala Yog.

Kamal Yog

Result : A person having Kamal Yog is famous, honourable, educated, healthy and able. He is expert in conversation and has longevity.

Vapi Yog

Definition : If there are marks of circles on nine out of ten fingers, then it is Vapi Yog.

Result : A person having the above yog rises from his ordinary circumstances. He is meek, virtuous, clever and happy.

Result : A person having the above combination goes abroad many times in his life and leads a fully respectable life in the society.

Result : A person having Vayu Yog engages himself in auspicious and religious works and earns respect in the society. He progresses continuously.

Result : A person who has the above combination, progresses in his life and goes abroad due to business.

Result : Those who have the above combination, get enough happiness in the middle and old ages of their lives. They get benefit from their officers and get high posts. Such persons follow the social traditions and customs fanatically.

Result : A person having the above combination is the keeper of animals. He becomes prosperous by the sale and purchase of animals and leads a happy life.

Gaj Yog

Navesh Yog

Definition : If there are five circles and three conchshell marks on both the hands, then it is Navesh Yog.

Result : A person born with the above combination has longevity, wealth, virtues and great fortune. He gets sudden wealth many times in his life.

Note : The marks of circle and conchshell should be seen on the first phalanges of the fingers of hands.

Kalnidhi Yog

Definition : If there are in all four circles and four conchshell marks in fingers of both the hands, then the person has Kalnidhi Yog.

Result : A person having such a combination has a high standard of life. He has good temper and is a high officer. He is also a fortunate person.

Kalnidhi Yog
