Definition : If the life line, fate line, health line and the Sun line are clear and prominent, then it is Ashta Laxmi Yog.

Result : A person having the above combination is the owner of unparallelled wealth. All his material desires are fulfilled and he is respected in the society. Such a person travels abroad and is honoured abroad as well as at home. He is quite a lucky person.

Ashta Laxmi Yog

The Lines of the Forehead

Along with the detailed study of the lines of the palm, it is necessary for a palmist to study the other parts of the body of a person and to think of their results in his heart. It helps in the comprehensive study of the personality of the incumbent. It also helps in authentic and correct fortune-telling.

The Line of Forehead

Our ancient saints have described seven points on the forehead. The lines and their corresponding planets are :

The first line below the hair on the forehead is controlled by the Saturn.

The second line below it is controlled by the Jupiter.

The third line is controlled by the Mars.

The fourth line is controlled by the Sun.

The fifth line is controlled by the Venus.

The sixth line is controlled by the Mercury, and

The seventh line, which is below all, is controlled by the Moon.

The signs of Zodiac and their location on the forehead :

1. Aries ts shape is like that of a goat and its position is above the left ear.

2. Taurus Its shape is like the figure of four in Hindi () and its location is the centre of the forehead.

3. Gemini Its sign is two perpendicular lines (∥). Its location is on the upper parts of the left eye.

4. Cancer Its sign is double seven in Hindi. One seven is straight and the other is reverse. Its location is on the upper part of the forehead.

5. Leo Its sign is circular form of the vowel u () in Hindi. Its location is on the right side of the forehead.

6. Virgo Its sign is the combination of N and P. Its location is right cheek.

7. Libra A straight line below and a bow above it is the sign. Its location is outside the right ear.

8. Scorpio Its shape is like M. Its location is above the mouth.

9. Sagittarius The shape is like the bow of a date tree. It is visible on the upper parts of the right eye.

10. Capricorn Its shape is like the alphabet V and P of English. It is visible at the chin.

11. Aquarius Two slanting lines depict Acquarius. Its location is on the left side of the forehead.

12. Pisces Its shape is like 36 () of Hindi figure. Its location is on the left cheek.

Signs of Planets and their Location on the Forehead

1. Sun Its sign is circle with a centre. Its location is the right eye.

2. Moon Its sign is like a bow. Its location is the left eye.

3. Mars The sign of Mars is three prongs. It is visible on the upper part of the head.

4. Mercury Its sign is a slanting line on a perpendicular line. It is based on the chin.

5. Jupiter Its sign is the shape of 2 (2). Its location is the right ear.

6. Venus Its sign is a circle around a plus mark (+). It is based on the nose.

7. Saturn Its shape is like the sign of vowel e () in Hindi. It is located on the left ear.

The Results of the Lines of the Forehead

The chief seven lines and their location on the forehead has already been dealt with in the previous pages. If one ventures to study minutely, one finds that the small line above the right eye is the line of the Sun. Similarly, the line of Moon is located above the left eye, the line of Venus is between the eyebrows and the line of Mercury is located on the forepart of the nose.

The following are their results :

If the line of Jupiter is curved and circular at the centre of the forehead, then the incumbent suffers from miseries.

If the line of Jupiter is curved in the centre and straight at the end, then the person earns respect.

If the line of Saturn is curved, then the person has the habit of indulgence.

A person having three straight, simple and clear lines is con- sidered lucky.

If the line of Jupiter is small and the line of Saturn is broken, then the person is virtuous, respectable and anxious.

If the line of Jupiter is of the shape of a snake, then the person indulges in dallience.

If there are many broken lines on the forehead of a person, then he is unlucky and remains ill.

If the line of Mars is small, then the person is poor.

If the lines of Jupiter and Mars have broken in between, then the person always lacks wealth.

If the lines of Saturn and Jupiter are bow-shaped, then the person is wicked by nature.

If the line of Saturn is very long and deep, then one comes into contact with other women.

If the line of Mars is snake-shaped, then the person is a murderer.

One who has just one line, is always wandering and is mean by nature.

If the branches come out of the line of Jupiter, then the person is a liar and wicked.

If there are four lines on the forehead, then the person is of good character and intelligent.

If the lines of Jupiter, Saturn and Mars are broken, then the person is unlucky.

If there are many small lines on the forehead below the hair, then he dies due to drowning.

If the lines of Saturn and Mars are broken and the line of Jupiter is inclined below, then the person is lucky and wealthy.

If the lines of Jupiter and Saturn have joined each other, then the person dies due to hanging.

If the line of Saturn is very deep and is inclined, then the person is a murderer.

If there is only one snake-shaped line, then the person is very strong.

If the lines of Mars and Saturn are shaped like the hood of a snake, then the person dies due to hanging.

If the line of Saturn is flexible, the line of Jupiter is inclined and the line of the Sun is long, then the person has longevity, virtues and fortune.

If the line of the Sun is small and the line of Venus is long, then the person is of good character, clever and lucky.

If the line of Saturn is small, the line of Jupiter is broken and the line of Mars has branches, then the person is a murderer.

If the line of the Sun is broken in the middle, then the person is wrathful, quarrelsome and indulges in dallience.

If the Sun line is curved, then the person is harsh by nature.

If the Sun line is bow-shaped and the line of Venus is broken in the centre, then the person is meek, wealthy and lover of fine arts.

If the lines of Mars and Sun are snake-like, then the person is without wealth.

If the line of Saturn is long and the line of Mars is snake-shaped, then the person is religious, kind and lives in high society.

If the lines of Saturn and Jupiter are half-moon-shaped in the above part, then the person is very fortunate.

If there is mark of a trident between the two eyebrows, then surely he gets one of his organs cut.

If the lines of Saturn and Jupiter are snake-shaped, then the person is deceitful by nature.

If the snake-shaped line of Jupiter reaches the line of the Saturn, then the person is quarrelsome.

If the line of Saturn is thin and the line of Jupiter is thick and long, then that person kills other persons.

If the line of Mars is inclined and the line of Venus is cut on the right, then the person is proud, wrathful and loves other women.

If the line of Saturn is deep and there are enough hair between the two eyebrows, then the person gets married more than once and is wealthy.

If the line of Jupiter is long and flexible, then he is a lucky and handsome person.

If the lines of Saturn and Jupiter are bow-like, then the person is brave.

The Line of Saturn

If the line of Saturn is straight, then the person is wise; if it is curved, then he is of irritating nature.

The Line of Jupiter

If it is straight, then the person is honest; if it is curved or broken, then he indulges himself in illegal works.

The Line of Mars

If it is straight, then the person gets success in every work; if it is curved, then he gets failure in his works.

The Line of Sun

If it is straight, then the person is intelligent and gets success in his life; if it is curved, then he is greedy and miserly.

The Line of Moon

If it is straight, then the person is wise and clever and observes minutely; if it is curved, then he is weak-minded.

The Line of Venus

If this line is straight, then the person proceeds on a truthful path and enjoys all sorts of happiness. If it is curved, he gets a bad name in love affairs.

The Line of Mercury

If it is straight, then the person is a successful orator and influences others. If it is curved, then the person is a liar and cheats others.

If there is a trident on the forehead, then the person has longevity.

One who has the mark of an oyster shell, is a teacher and an ideal person.

If there are blue nerves visible on the forehead, then he is a sinner.

If there is a benedictory mark on the forehead, then the person is a multi-millionaire.

If the forehead is small, then the person is unintelligent and poor.

If the forehead is high, then the person leads the life of a king.

If the forehead is circular, then he is a miser.

If there is a half-moon on the forehead, then he is a famous industrialist.

If there is the sign of a thunderbolt or a bow on the forehead, then he is the owner of unparallelled wealth.

If there is sign of a trident and conchshell on the forehead of a person, then he is considered fortunate.

Points on the Forehead and its Results

There are two sorts of points found on the forehead; the black points and the red points. The red point is considered auspicious and the black point is considered inauspicious. Sometimes, the black point is also good.

If there is a red point on the right side of the line of Saturn, then the person is laborious and wealthy. If there is black point, then he is clever.

If there is a red point above the line of Saturn, then the person has a special affinity for women. He also does his work completely. If there is black point, then he gets a bad name in a woman's love.

If there is a black point on the middle or below the line of Saturn, then the person is a coward. If the point is red, the result remains the same.

If there is a black point on the left side of the line of Saturn, then he travels many times in his life. If the point is red, then he earns wealth by these travels.

If there is a point on the right of the line of Jupiter, then the person progresses. The result remains the same for a red point also.

If there is a red or black point in the centre of the forehead over the line of Jupiter, then the person is intelligent and clever.

If there is red or black point on the left of Jupiter, then the person remains happy in his life from all angles.

If there is a red or a black point on the right side of the line of Mars, then the person is famous, wealthy and happy.

If there is a point on the middle of the line of Mars, then the person is issueless. Both the points, red or black, have the same results here.

If there is a point on the left side of the line of Mars, then such a person is quarrelsome and is also a dare-devil. Here red or black points indicate the same results.

If there is a point on the right side of the line of Apollo, then the incumbent gets benefit from land or landed property.

If there is a point in the middle of the line of Apollo, then the person is prosperous, happy and famous.

If there is a point on the left side of the line of Apollo, then the person's domestic life is always full of problems.

If there is a black or red point on the right side of the line of Venus, then his domestic life is very happy.

If there is a red or black point on the line of Venus on the forehead, then that person is very happy and prosperous materially.

If there is a red or black point on the left side of the line of Venus, then the person is lascivious or makes love with other women.

If there is a red or black point on the right side of the line of Mercury, then the person is a successful businessman.

If there is a black or red point on the left side of the line of Mercury, then such a person is a coward and fearful and spoils his work himself.

If there is a point in the centre of the line of Mercury, then the person is foresighted and prosperous.

If there is a point on the right side of the line of Luna, then such a person becomes famous in the society and is prosperous.

If there is a red or black point on the line of Luna, then such a person is of short age and has genital diseases.

If there is a red or black point on the left side of the line of Luna, then such a person troubles others.

If there is a point on the temple above the left ear, then the whole life of the incumbent is spent in misery.

If there is a point near the eyebrow of the left eye, then such a person loves loneliness and leads a simple life.

If there is a mark near the eyebrow, then such persons are considered unsuccessful.

If there is a point on the right temple of the forehead, then such a person is loving, prosperous and leads a happy life.

If there is a point near the right ear, then that person is courageous.

If there is a point on the right eyebrow, then his eyes are weak.

If there is a point near the right nostril, then the person is wealthy, happy and successful.

If there is a point below the right eye, then the person is happy and prosperous.

If there is a point in the centre of the nose, then the person is travel-minded and cruel by nature.

If there is a point on the left side of the nose, then the person has to labour hard to achieve success.

If there is a point on the upper lip, then the person is very licentious and is fond of women.

If there is a point on the lower lip, then the person is poor and spends his life in poverty.

If there is a point on the chin, then the person engages himself in his own works and is almost selfish.

If there is a point on the neck, then such persons are wise and gather wealth by their efforts.

If there is a point on the left cheek, then the person lacks wealth but his domestic life is generally happy.

If there is a point on the right cheek, then the person is wise and makes progress.

If there is a point on top corner of the left ear, then the person has longevity but has weak body.

If there is a point on the top corner of the right ear, then the person is of simple nature and makes progress in his youth.

If there is a point on the head, then the person is rich.

If there is a point on the right part of the head, then the person is very much honoured in the society.

If there is a point on the left part of the head, then the person faces troubles throughout his life.

If there is a point between both the eyebrows, then the persons have longevity and are religious and liberal.

If there is a point below or above the eye, then the person is wealthy, wise and clever.

If there is a red point on the cheeks, then they are wealthy but they squander their money by their foolishness.

If there is a red point in the right palm, then the person is wealthy.

If there is a point in the left hand, then the person spends money wisely.

Hence, while studying the lines of hand, one should study the points found on the face and elsewhere, so that one may know fully well about the personality of the incumbent.

Body Marks

Along with the study of the lines of hand, one should have knowledge about the body of an incumbent. As soon as one sees the body of a person, one knows nearly half of his fortune.

In the following lines, I am dealing briefly with general marks of the body of a person :

Ears

If the ears are protruded and their ends are prominent and big, then the person is considered fortunate.

If the ears are long since birth, then he is happy.

One whose ears are thick, is tender by nature.

One whose ears are small, is intelligent.

One whose ears are conch-like, reaches a high office in the army.

A flat-eared person is lascivious.

Ears having long hair forecast longevity.

Very large ears indicate leadership qualities.

Very small ears indicate miserly habits.

Dry ears indicate poverty.

Large and fully spread ears indicate cruel nature.

A person having large ears is honourable.

Ears without greasiness indicate weakness.

A woman having hair on ears, becomes a widow.

Large ears of a woman are considered good.

SHAPE OF NOSE

If the nose is four fingers long, then it indicates longevity.

A person having protruded nose, is of good character.

A person having an elephant-like nose, is lascivious.

A person having a parrot-like nose, is happy.

A person having a straight nose, is fortunate.

A person having small nostrils, is lucky.

A person having inclined front portion of nose, is prosperous economically.

A pointed nose indicates kingship.

A small nose indicates religious personality.

If the tip of the nose of a person is divided into two parts, then he is a poor person.

A flat-nosed person is simple by nature.

A cut-nose is of a sinner.

A nose inclined to the right, indicates weakness.

Big nostrils are considered best.

If the nose of a woman is small, then she is laborious by nature.

A flat and long nose of a woman indicates widowhood.

If the tip of the nose of a woman is long, then she enjoys the happiness of a queen.

If there is a black point or a wart, then the woman is of loose character.

A very long nose of a woman indicates unhappiness.

A shapely and equally-holed nose indicates decency.

The Mouth

SHAPE OF FACE

If the mouth is small, then it is considered good.

If the mouth is wide-spread, then it indicates poverty.

If the mouth has width then it is considered inauspicious.

The Neck

A small neck indicates good fortune.

A round and strong neck indicates wealth.

A person having conch-shaped neck, is a king.

A person having buffalo-like big neck, is strong.

A person having a bullock-like neck, has short age.

A long-necked person is lascivious.

An inclined-necked person is a back-biter.

A flat and long-necked person is miserable.

A neck without flesh indicates poverty.

Four-fingers long neck is considered the best. Its circumference should be between 24 and 26 fingers.

A neck showing and having big bones indicates poverty.

If the beads of the neck of a woman are straight, then she has longevity.

If the bead is high, then she is considered lucky.

A fleshy and beautiful neck is considered the best.

A woman having neck with three lines, is wealthy.

A woman whose bones of the neck are visible, is considered unlucky.

A thick-necked woman becomes a widow.

If the nerves of the neck are visible, then the woman is poor.

If the neck of a woman is very long, then she brings a bad name on her family.

A woman is considered the best if she has a beautiful, shapely and four-fingered neck.

The Chin

If the chin is round and fleshy, then that person is rich.

Long, thin and weak chin indicates poverty.

If the jaws are round, then they are considered auspicious.

If the front of the chin is beautiful and tender, then it is considered auspicious.

If the front part of the chin is reddish, then it is considered inauspicious.

If the chin of a woman measures two fingers and if it is fleshy and beautiful, then the woman is fortunate.

If there are hair on the chin of a woman, then she is of loose character.

The Cheeks

If the cheeks are plumpy, then the person is happy.

Fleshy cheeks indicate amorous dalliance.

Those who have cheeks bulging like a lion, are kings.

Cheeks without flesh and squeezed, indicate miserable conditions.

A person having plumpy cheeks is a minister.

Women having clean and beautiful cheeks are considered the best.

Those women who have hair on their cheeks, lead an unhappy life.

If there are no nerves visible on the cheeks of a woman, then she is considered like a goddess.

Women having dimples in their cheeks, are materialistic and love many things.

Lips

Persons having red lips, are rich.

Persons having pink lips, are intelligent.

Persons having big lips, are religious.

Persons having long lips, love amorous dalliance.

A person haring zig-zag lips, leads an unhappy life.

Dry, thin and ugly lips indicate poverty.

A woman, who has red and greasy lips, is the best.

If there is a line between the lips of a woman, then she is considered fortunate.

Curved and zig-zag lips of a woman denote hard luck.

If a woman has black and thick lips, then she is denied the happiness of a husband.

If the lips of woman are very big, then she likes quarrelling.

If the upper lip is tender, inclined and greasy, then the woman is a fortunate one.

If the lower lip of a woman is protruded upwards, then she is a widow.

A woman having round and reddish lips, gets full happiness of a husband.

Teeth

If the teeth of a person are in a straight line, are equitably protruded and are greasy, then he is a rich person.

Persons having long teeth, are wealthy.

If the teeth of a person are inclined inside, then he is a poor person.

Persons having black and rugged teeth suffer from difficulties.

A person having thirty-two teeth is fortunate.

A person having thirty teeth remains worried for wealth.

A person having thirty-one teeth loves amorous dalliance.

Persons having less teeth always remain poor.

Those persons whose teeth fall gradually, have longevity.

If the teeth of a person are separate from one another, then he enjoys the wealth of others.

If the teeth of a woman are sharp, white, straight and are joined with each other, then she is a fortunate one.

Those women who have sixteen teeth on each side (upper and lower sides) and are as white as cow's milk, are too much liked by their husbands.

Those women who have small teeth, live miserably.

Those who have more teeth on the lower jaw, do not get the happiness of their mothers.

Zig-zag teeth of a woman indicate widowhood.

A woman having white gums, is crooked.

Thick and terrible teeth of a woman indicate misery.

If the teeth are separate and have gaps, then the woman is of loose character.

If a woman has a tooth over another tooth, then she is clever, selfish and commands her husband.

Those who have black gums, are thieves.

The Tongue

If a man has a red, thin and tender tongue, then he is very wise, clever and a devotee of God.

One whose tongue is sharp in front and reddish, enjoys all the happiness of life.

One whose tongue is whitish, is a scoundrel.

Black or blue tongue indicates poverty.

Thick and equitably wide tongue or a yellow tongue indicates foolishness of a person.

One who can touch the tip of the nose with his tongue, is a great ascetic or a devotee.

A person having a long tongue, is blunt.

A person having a wide tongue, squanders money.

Those women who have tender, red and thin tongues, are fortunate.

Those women who have narrow tongues, are considered inauspicious.

A woman who has a thick tongue, does not enjoy full age.

A woman having a reddish tongue, weds the best husband.

A woman having a black tongue, is quarrelsome.

A woman having a very wide tongue, suffers from unhappiness continuously.

The Laughter

If the teeth do not come out when one laughs, then he is the best person.

Those persons who move their heads and shoulders when they laugh, love amorous dalliance or they are sinners.

Persons who laugh with closed eyes, are unreligious.

Those who always smile, progress continuously in their lives.

That woman who does not show teeth while laughing and opens her mouth just a little, is a fortunate one.

While laughing if a woman trembles repeatedly or she laughs with full-throated ease, loves amour and other persons.

If there are dimples in the cheeks of a woman while she laughs, she desires other men.

The Tone (For ladies only)

If the voice of a woman is like a lute, then she is of the best quality.

A woman having cuckoo-like voice is fortunate.

A woman whose voice is like a peacock, weds a wealthy person.

Hoarse voice indicates misery.

If the voice of a woman is trembling, then she is unhappy.

Special Features (For women)

A woman having black and long pupils of eyes, is considered the best.

A woman having small and black hair on the eye-lids, is considered lucky.

A woman having deer-like eyes, is considered as having auspicious signs.

A woman having round or cat-like eyes, is considered crooked.

A woman whose both the eyes are yellowish, is lascivious.

A woman having reddishness in both the eyes, likes to cohabit with other persons.

A woman having both the eyes full of water, is considered perfect.

A woman having eyes like men, or if her eyes are inset, is considered fickle.

A woman who stares while looking at others, is fraudulent.

A woman who presses her left eye while talking, is licentious.

One who presses her right eye while talking, has small number of issues.

A woman having arch-like brows, is considered auspicious.

A woman having brows with rough hair, is inauspicious.

Those women who do not have brows, are poor.

Those who have thick eyebrows, remain entangled with other men.

A woman having long hair on eye brows, remains issueless.

Those women who have black spot or a wart on the left cheek, are considered auspicious.

A woman having a black point on the neck, is bestowed with a son in the first instance.

One who has beautiful nails, is kind.

One having big eyes, broad breasts and thin waist, gets honour in the society.

A woman having long and thin fingers, has longevity.

A woman having three lines on the neck, becomes prosperous.

A woman who has long and thick lips, cheats her husband.

A woman who has blackish nails and lips, does not have good character.

While sleeping if a woman lets down saliva, then she is unchaste and spoils the family honour.

If, while laughing, there are dimples in the cheeks of a woman and if her eyes move, then she is licentious.

A woman having a very small mouth, cheats her husband.

A woman having very long mouth, is poor.

A woman, who grinds her teeth while sleeping, leads a poor life.

A woman having small eyes, is considered inauspicious.

A woman having round and equitable head, lives longer.

A woman having four lines on the forehead, is fortunate.

One who has three lines on the forehead, has longevity.

A woman having only one line, is inauspicious.

A woman, whose soles of feet are smooth and equal, gets happiness.

A woman having rough and hard soles, is unfortunate.

Those women who make thudding sound while walking, are foolish.

Those who have marks of conchshell, flag or fish, marry a multi-millionaire.

One whose feet has ‘urdhwa' line, enjoys happiness.

One whose toe is fleshy and round, gives happiness.

If the toe is long, she is unlucky.

One whose toe is short, is lucky.

One whose fingers of feet are tender and joined together, get auspicious results.

One whose fingers of feet are long, is licentious.

If the fingers of feet are thin, then she is poor.

One who has curved fingers of feet, is crooked.

One who has flat fingers of feet, leads a servant-like life.

If there is gap between the fingers of feet, then she is poor.

While walking if a woman allows sand to go up, then she is licentious and gets infamy.

If the smallest finger of the foot does not touch the ground while a woman walks, then she is attached to other person.

If two fingers of the feet of a woman do not touch the earth, then she cheats her husband.

If the upper part of the foot is tender, smooth and fleshy, then she is fortunate.

If the ankle-joints of a woman are round, then she is auspicious.

If the ankle-joints are loose below, then it indicates misfortune.

If the heel of a woman is broad, then it indicates misfortune for her.

A woman having round, smooth and hairless thighs, is like a queen.

A woman whose both the knees are round and fleshy, is rich.

A woman whose calves of the legs are like trunk of an elephant, is considered the best.

If a woman has long hair on the calves of the legs, then she becomes a widow very soon.

Those who have flat calves of the legs, are unlucky.

Those whose calves have thick skin, are poor.

A woman having thin waist (24 fingers), is considered the best.

Long and flat waist gives misery.

A woman having hairy waist, becomes a widow.

A woman having broad buttocks, is lascivious.

If the buttocks are round, tender and fleshy, then she is auspicious.

A woman who has a deep and lined navel, provides wealth.

A woman whose navel is upwards and the centre is clearly visible, is inauspicious.

One whose ribs are tender and fleshy, enjoys happiness of life.

One who has hair on the ribs, is ill-natured.

A woman whose stomach is small and has tender skin, is of good nature.

A woman having a pot-like stomach is poor.

If the stomach is much broad, then she is unlucky.

A woman having a long stomach, brings calamity on the father-in-law or on the elder brother-in-law.

One who has three lines on the stomach, is considered lucky.

One whose hair are straight and thin, enjoys happiness.

One whose hair line is wayward, becomes a widow.

One whose breast is hairless, is loved by her husband.

One whose breast is wide, is cruel.

A woman should have 36 fingers wide breast which is also auspicious.

If the breast is hard, round and firm, then it is considered good.

If the breast is big and dry, then it indicates misery.

If the right breast of a woman is upward, then she is lucky.

If both the breasts of a woman are suppressed, then she is licentious.

If the front of the breast is black and round, then it indicates auspicious sign.

If the collar bone of a woman is big, then she is lucky.

If the collar bone is loose, then she is poor.

If the shoulders are leaning, then she is auspicious.

If the shoulders are curved, high and hairy, then she becomes a widow.

If the shoulders are leaning forward and firm, then she enjoys life.

If her arms are tender, straight and without hair, then she is auspicious.

If the arms are full of hair, then she becomes a widow.

If the arms are small, then they withstand misery.

If the palm is reddish and without holes, then she is lucky.

If there are a number of lines or number of nerves on the palm, then she is poor.

If the nails are red and protruded, then she is considered the best.

Yellow nails indicate poverty.

White spots on the nails indicate licentiousness.

If the back is leaning, then she withstands misery.

If there is thick growth of hair on the back of a woman, then she becomes a widow.

A woman having straight looks, is holy.

A woman, who looks below, is criminal.

If both the eyes are very near, then she cheats.

If both the eyes are far, then she is foolish.

If there is a black mark on the forehead, then she enjoys throughout her life.

If there is a black mark over the heart, then she is lucky.

If there is a black mark on the right breast, then that woman gives birth to a greater number of girls.

If there is red point on the left breast, then she becomes widow.

If there is red point on the front portion of the nose of a woman, then she is very loving to her husband.

If there is a black point on the front of the nose, then she is of bad character.

If there is a point below the navel, then it is auspicious.

If there is a black mark on the left hand, then she is lucky.

If a woman has black marks on lips, hands, ears or neck, then she enjoys her life throughout.

The Twenty-one Types of Women

There are twenty-one types of women. Their description is as follows :

1. Padmini : Such a woman is kind and loving. She is also attractive. She walks like a goose and serves her parents. She emits lotus-like smell from her body. She is beautiful, impressive and serves her husband. Her nose, ears and lips are small. She has conch-shell-shaped neck and has a face like a lotus. Such a woman is lucky, faithful and has less number of issues.

2. Chitrani : Such women are faithful and affectionate. They love to beautify themselves. They do not labour much but they are intelligent. They have a round head and active eyes. They walk like elephants and speak like peacocks. Such women have tender organs and are shy. Such women keep their husbands happy by their dances, love and beauty.

3. Hastini : They work according to the wishes of men. They love sex. They are fat and a little lazy. They do not have shame or religion. Their lips, nostrils, ears and neck are thick. Their eyes are small and yellowish. Their lips are thick and large and they walk like elephants. They become angry soon and are quarrelsome. They are not satisfied with their husbands and are always eager to have relationship with other persons.

4. Shankhini : They are tall and when they walk, they make thudding sound. They move their buttocks while walking. Their eyes are curved and their bodies are unshapely. They have a great feeling of wrath. They are eager to have sex every moment. They are callous by heart and love to use intoxicants. They are licentious and keep engaged with other men.

5. Sadmini : They are of easy-natured and timid. Such women are shy and cheerful. Their voice is sweet and they know the art of making their husbands happy by all means.

6. Metrayani : They are beautiful, fair-coloured, attractive and work with acting but their husbands are cruel and weak. So their domestic life is not happy. Everybody is attracted by their beauty but they keep away from other men.

7. Kalah Karini : The eyebrows of such a woman are always straining. Her teeth are irregular. She looks like a buffalo while she walks. She allows sand to go up. She keeps grudge and kills her husband by fraud. She considers it her cleverness to establish relationship with other persons.

8. Grihasthini : Such a woman runs the domestic life ideally. She remains attached to her husband. She neither speaks more nor does she cheat anyone. She neither love other person, nor does she believe in beautifying herself. Such a woman abstains herself from all sorts of evils and gets high honour for both of her houses.

9. Atura : Such a woman believes in performing all acts quickly. She is ordinarily beautiful and loves her husband but sometimes she quarrels fiercely with her husband. To understand the nature of such a woman is very tideous.

10. Bhayatura : She is fair-coloured, delicate and shy in talking. She never lives alone. She loves everybody, speaks softly and follows her religion.

11. Dakini : She laughs while talking and is very clever in cheating. She has reddish eyes. Outwardly, she shows great affection but inwardly she is greedy and cheats others. Loving such a woman is like loving a snake. The husband of such a woman remains miserable due to her bad name.

12. Hansini : She is happy and understanding. She walks beautifully like a she-goose. She speaks the truth and is beautiful like goddess of love. She loves affectionately. Only lucky persons are bestowed with such women.

13. Bahu Vanshini : Such a woman is wheatish in colour. She likes to lead a fully domestic life faithfully. She does not tell a lie and remains happy in the service of her husband. She is honoured in the society.

14. Kripani : Such a woman is shameless, weak and miser. She is a bit bad-named. She does not feel shy in discussing any matter with anyone. She can sleep with anyone if she gets money.

15. Ghatini : She is very clever and is adept in cheating. Outwardly, she shows affection but to the core of her heart, she is full of venom. She tells lies and is very adept in cheating.

16. Premini : She is beautiful, loving and is loved by her husband. She is always smiling and can give anything to her well-wisher and benefactor. Her hair is long and beautiful and her face is attractive.

17. Krishtanvi : She is lean and thin and angry by nature, she thinks herself to be very clever. She trembles while speaking.

18. Madmastini : She is very proud and sex-loving. She always defeats men-folk in love-making. She is very licentious and lives like a society girl. She never likes to settle at one place.

19. Kulchhedini : In whichever house she goes, she gives it poverty. She loves sinning. She cheats her husband very often. She does not care for the honour of her parents, husband, brother and in-laws. She is almost licentious.

20. Naraki : Such a woman has small eyes and likes to sin. She excites others to quarrel. Such a woman cheats and tells lies.

21. Swargini : Such a woman has pious thoughts, religious bias and gentle in behaviour with everybody. She devotes to godly feelings. She respects all youngers or elders. She is soft-spoken and is honoured in the society. She honours her husband more than God.

I have dealt with some of the special features of women in the above lines. There are other types like Manini, Dharki, Dushta, Pataki, etc. In all, there are 64 types. Out of these, the above 21 types are important.

Practical Knowledge of Palmistry

There are facts regarding the lines of hand in the books of Palmistry but none of them has the practical knowledge, due to which the students of Palmistry are able to learn theory of it but have no practical knowledge.

In this book, an attempt is being made for the first time to impart practical knowledge. The hand of an unknown woman was put before Dr. Shrimali. He studied the hand and analysed the following facts :

The hand which was shown to Dr. Shrimali has been printed on this very page. He was sent an imprint of the hand which had neither the name of the woman nor her identity was disclosed to him.

Dr. Shrimali studied the hand and gave a detailed account of it.

Keeping in view the fact that he had given a completely true forecasting, I took my acquaintee, a woman, to his residence and requested him that 1 was coming from the publisher and told him that at the end of his published work, I wanted to give an appendix on practical knowledge of Palmistry. So, Dr. Shrimali should kindly study the lady's hand and give information about it.

Whatever forecast Dr. Shrimali predicted is as follows. Readers should also learn it through Palmistry. The hand-print of the lady is being given here. It is to be kept in view that when the concerned lady and I met Dr. Shrimali, I did not give any information about the lady nor the lady's name, nor did Dr. Shrimali ask us anything about her.

As soon as Dr. Shrimali touched the hand, he said that the mounts of Jupiter, Saturn, Sun and Mercury were fully developed. “It is a tender, soft and ideal hand. It indicates that the lady belongs to a cultured family and that there is a very congenial and civilized atmosphere in her father's as well as her husband's house. The lady is cultured and religious.”

“It is a beautiful and fateful hand but the Sun line has been cut, though it is straight. So it indicates that her education is very ordinary. Though there were great efforts to educate her yet she got very ordinary education. At the most, she got education upto class eighth though she appears to be very educated and learned and it appears that she is atleast a graduate. The lines of a hand do not tell lies and it is evident that she got continuous obstructions in her education and so she must have acquired very ordinary education.

On studying the life line, many surprising facts came to light. The initial stage of the life line was that it was formed by many lines. So the first years of the childhood were of illness. “And when one looks minutely, it is deduced that in the 5th year of your life, you sufferred from a serious disease but the life line has been cut there and it has relationship with the head line, so it is very clear that there was some trouble and that you nearly escaped death. Isn't it correct?”

The lady nodded in affirmation and replied that she had fallen seriously ill in her fifth year and the members of her family had thought that she would not survive but she got well after a month's illness.

Studying the life line, Dr. Shrimali continued, “It has broken at two or three other places also, so it is also a fact that in the tenth year, there was some special event which took place. This event was like drowning,”

The lady replied, “At the age of ten, I had gone with my mother to a tank and slipped down in deep water. Had I not been saved by the by-standers there, I would have drowned.”

Panditji replied that for her future she need not worry. “The future life line is clear and straight and it has reached the bracelets, so there is no possibility of any accident and your full age is nearly 65 or 66 years of age.”

“Please wait a minute! Your full age is 65 years, 4 months and 21 days.”

The lady smiled gratefully and saw towards Panditji.

“On the life line, there were many lines coming from the mount of Venus and two of them have nearly cut the life line. Along with it, there are many small lines. All these obstructing lines indicate that you had troubles, obstacles and problems. In a way, your childhood was spent in poverty, struggles and troubles. Though your mother line and father line are complete, so they must have longevity, but there cannot be two opinions that your parents did not cooperate in your future life.”

“Though I affirm that your parents cooperated in bringing you up but after your marriage, they did not give you support. Isn't it true?”

The lady nodded in affirmative and said nothing. “Now, I am telling you about your age at the time of marriage.”

Panditji counted and told her that she was married at a tender age of 14. “On minute examination we know that your marriage line is straight, so you must have spent less part of your life at your parents' house, so as soon as you completed your 14 years, you were married. At the most, it is 14 years and one or two months over it when you were married. I am surprised how you got married at such a tender age? Are early marriages permissible in your society?”

The lady nodded in affirmative and told that she was married when she was 14 years and 1 month old.

Continuing the study of the lines, Panditji said that her husband must have been very ordinary in the beginning, i.e., her inlaw's house was equal in status with her parent's house. Economically and socially, the position of her husband was negligible in the society at the time of their marriage. Her hand contained Girdle of Venus, though it had not formed fully but it indicated that since she went to the house of her husband, she had to face trouble, mental-tensions and anxieties. Physically, she might not have been so perturbed but mentally, she might have sufferred tensions and troubles more than enough and she might not have had a respite of even a moment in those days.

The woman nodded in affirmative and asked, “How long will it continue?”

Dr. Shrimali laughed and repeated, “How long?” “It does not mean anything. At that time, she was crossing the age of 42. So that period ended when she was 31 years old. Such mental troubles continued for 15 years after her marriage. From the age of 14 to 31 years, she might have experienced mental tensions. She might not have got respite even for a moment. She did not get any cooperation from her husband then.

But her condition should have improved in her 32nd year of age. By that time, her husband might have established his position in the society and he must have been able to provide her with mental peace, comfort, love, affection and cooperation.

The head line has inclined down and has been cut in the centre, along with it a point is also visible. This point is exactly at the place where the head line has been cut. The period comes to 38th year of her life. So, it seems that she might have received a great mental shock at the 38th year of her life. It seems that she felt darkness all around her and she might have broken down mentally. It appears that from the 32nd year of age to the 38th year of her life, she must have felt her life mentally balanced but that incident must have disturbed her thoroughly. The place where this line has been cut, at that very point, a line coming from the Sun line has met it. So, it appears that the shock received by her might have been from her son. There cannot be two opinions that she received the shock of separation from her eldest son and the incident might have occurred in such a manner that it made her life topsy-turvy. Certainly, the incident indicated the death of her eldest son. The eyes of the lady sitting opposite Panditji were full of tears. She withdrew her hand. She kept silent for a minute or two, then again she stretched her hand before Panditji.

Continuing the study of the line of her palm, he said, “Now, in your future life, you will not face any such incident which may perturb you mentally, though it is true that your mount of Luna is suppressed in itself and is weak which indicates that you will face mental tension throughout. There is always one incident or the other which keeps your mental tensions alive. In my opinion, it will be more suitable for you to wear a pearl in a silver ring and continue to wear it in your future life also. The pearl will be helpful in mitigating your mental problems and troubles.

The lady nodded and inquired as to what sort of pearl she should wear.

Panditji replied, “You should wear the best quality pearl of Basra Lake in Pushya Nakshtra. The pearl should be worn in a silver ring and it will be better if she wears it in the little finger of the right hand.”

Panditji continued without moving his head and said, “It seems to me that your health line is chained in initial stage and many smaller lines have emanated out of it but all these lines have moved ahead downward in the palm. It cannot be termed auspicious. These lines indicate this fact that your health must have been very weak and that you must have always suffered from one disease or the other. In fact, right from the 20th year to the 41st year of your life, you must have been in poor health, thereby making it necessary for the doctor to come nearly daily. As in your health line, the last line moving downward indicates the 41st year. All these lines have relationship with the mount of Luna and the mount of Venus. Because of its relationship with the mount of Luna, it seems that your troubles were concerning your stomach including weakness, anaemia, gastric trouble, indigestion, etc. Because of its relationship with the mount of Venus, you might have sufferred also from Leucorrhoea and other feminine diseases. Is it true?”

The woman nodded with downcast eyes. “But henceforth, you will not have any trouble from the health point of view, though there may be minor stomach troubles but those also will lessen by wearing a pearl. Your future life seems good from the health point of view. In the 58th year of your life, one more branch has inclined downwards and it has established relationship with the head line, so you will again fall sick for 3 or 4 months at that time.”

“In the whole of your future life, you will not have any trouble concerning your health.”

“Let me also study your marriage line along with it. As I have already told you, you got favour from your husband at the age of 31 and it is also a fact that your husband must have attained social and economic progress at that time. From the 32nd year, till date you got economic favour and also social favour from your husband. When we study it further, we find that in your future life, your husband will make steady progress economically and socially, and you will get maximum happiness from him. You have no worry in this connection. In truth, no other line is as clear, simple and faultless as this line is. So, there cannot be two opinions that in the whole of your future life, you will get full happiness and honour from your husband. You can be called a very lucky lady that way.”

“Now I may also make my views clear on the line of issues, there are six lines visible in your hand. Out of these, four are thick and two are thin. It makes it clear that you will have six issues, out of which four will be sons and two will be daughters. You can yourself see that your first issue line is cut so you will not get happiness of your first issue. As I have already told you, you had to bear the loss of your first issue at the age of 38. In your future life, you will get happiness from three sons and two daughters.”

“Along with it, I must tell you that from worldly point of view, you will not lack in happiness from your issues but if you keep this point in view that they will always obey you and will be very obedient, then please remove this illusion from your mind. It is a fact that you have more than enough tolerance, so you will pull on with all of them, though there will be differences and you will have mental agony due to these.”

“At the age of 44, you will arrange marriage of a son and a daughter. At the 48th year, you will again arrange the marriage of a son. At the age of 54, a daughter's and at the age of 57, a son's marriage will be arranged by you. You will get special happiness from your present second and third sons. Similarly, your youngest daughter will keep you mentally satisfied the most.”

“In your future life, you will have happiness from your issues. Your issues will be capable and they will attain height in their fields. So, from this point of view, you will not be at any loss.”

“After counting, it becomes evident that at the age of 47, you will become both grandmother and maternal grandmother. I mean to suggest that you will become a grandmother in that very year.”

The woman's face beamed for a moment or two but the next moment she became shy with downcast eyes.

“The mount of Jupiter is the best in itself in your hand, though the Jupiter is inclining towards the Saturn but the mount of Jupiter is faultless and clear. It indicates that you have led a pious life. You incline more towards religious aspects than material aspects of your life. It is your hearty wish that you want to do more and more religious works. You want to serve the cows, the Brahmins and the saints and go on pilgrimages.”

“But such is possible only after attaining the age of 36 years. It is also a fact that these religious thoughts will continue in your heart in your future life. As far as possible, you will be able to give alms and will do many other good deeds. Pilgrimages are also likely to continue after the age of 36 years. You will not lack any such thing though due to inclination towards the Saturn, there are always many domestic obstructions. But in one way or the other, these will continue in your life.”

“The mount of Saturn is firm on its position and the finger of Saturn has also its full length, so you must be a fortunate woman. You were the cause of progress of your father till you stayed there and you have been the cause of constant progress of your husband since you stepped into his house. I don't feel shy in telling you that the future economic and social progress of your husband will be a clue to the support of your mount of Saturn. So, your husband will continue progressing till he is in your contact or till both of you have congenial relationship. You will also get honour due to it.”

“The mount of Sun is also strong in your hand, but it has inclined towards the mount of Saturn and also the line of Sun is composed of many other lines. (Though the allied lines provide force to the line of the Sun but some of these lines are cutting it as well). So it is obvious in your life that you may do whatever amount of work, you will not get any credit for it. If I may tell you the truth, you did not get any credit at your father's house nor did you get any credit at your in-laws' house. Though it is a fact that you will always serve everybody in your in-law's family, you will also help them from time to time. You will also keep sweet and favourable behaviour with them. But when need be, you will neither get any cooperation nor any credit from them. By studying your hand, it is clear that you got your father-in-law's love for 14 years after your marriage but your mother-in-law has longevity. But there cannot be two opinions that you got obstructions, troubles, obstacles and mental tensions from them. The more you tried to give them pleasures and happinesses, the more they troubled you physically and mentally. This fact is also true with your brother-in-law, sister-in-law, mother-in-law, your brother and your parents. What I mean to suggest is that you will always try to help them but they will not give you any credit nor any honour, nor will you find these from them in your future life.

Instead, you will go on helping them and will try to give them comfort and happiness till you die.

You must have noted it in your hand that one line starts from the mount of Jupiter and meets the line of Sun. So, whatever cooperation and honour you will get, it will be from your husband. You will get the love and cooperation of your husband throughout your life and this fact will mitigate your mental tensions.

So far as the mount of Mercury is concerned, it is generally good. But it has enlarged outside your palm and also the line of Mercury has enlarged towards the little finger. It makes it clear that you are a minute observer and a wise lady. This is also true that you can understand the true character of any unknown person or any lady, also his standard and whether he will be helpful or not. All these things are clear in your mind in the fraction of a second. You might have also felt that whatever you think about them proves to be quite true.

Along with it, this mount of Mercury informs that you are endowed with the godly gift that many times you know about the coming events beforehand and you might have also felt that those events were quite true. It is because of your pure and sinless heart. This fact also reveals that you are a blessed one. On this mount of Mercury, one line meets it (coming from the mount of the Moon). So, there is a clear indication of your visit abroad. The fate line relationship is also clear here. So, you may go abroad in the 41st, 43rd and 46th years of your life. The last two visits out of these will be important and favourable. Most of these will be with your husband but in the 56th year, you may go abroad with your son.

You will be able to see most of Europe and parts of America by these visits. Whereas the duration of the visits is 1 to 4 months, these will be beneficial to you from the health (both mental and physical) point of view.

You will also visit the whole of India in your life and will proceed on pilgrimages many times in your life.

The mount of Moon is constrained and is slipping inside the palm. So, this mount will give you mental tension and troubles. As I have already told you, you will keep on suffering from gastric trouble and indigestion, etc. Whatever mental tensions you have sufferred, the main reason for them was the mount of Moon on your palm. I have already given you the remedy for it earlier.

The Dragon's Head is suppressed in your hand. It is a favour in itself. It has not allowed you to have irreligious or immoral thoughts and feelings, nor is it possible in your future life. In a way, its suppression has helped in giving height to your religious feelings.

The bracelets are favourable for you. The first bracelet is chained which gives profits. The three bracelets in your hand give you longevity.

The mount of Venus is also favourable. The life line is bow-shaped and it has given dimensions to the mount of Venus. The obstructing lines on it indicate obstacles in your life about which I have already informed you. The mount of Venus has protruded near the palm which indicates that there is a happy combination of material and spiritual devotion. In a way, the tendencies of pleasure have a special effect of religious tendencies in your life and it is favourable in your life.”

“Your thumb has full length and is drawn back. It shows your firmness of will. This thumb also indicates that you remain firm on your views in your life. Once whatever you decide, you try to fulfil it, come what may.”

“The first phalange of the thumb is long and firm in itself. This firmness helps in developing logic in you. It also appears that you do not work with closed eyes, instead you either rectify it or try to give it newness. By looking at this phalange of your thumb, it appears that you like criticism of others a little. If a work has been done by anyone other than you, then either you will try to find fault in it or you will not hesitate in giving this suggestion that the work would have been done better the other way.

The root of the thumb, where the mount of Venus has started developing, is protruded. The protrusion leads you towards materialism. It has been your innermost feeling to live grandly and decently. You also have a little love for exhibition in your heart.

A horizontal line from the mount of Venus has gone to the mount of Mars. It has cut all the obstructing lines. If we compare it with your age line, then it comes to the age of 43. Mars is the owner of land and Venus is that of material happiness. Therefore, it so seems that by the end of the 43rd year and at the beginning of the 44th year of your life, you will have your own house. It is different that you neither serve anywhere nor do you have any independent source of income, but this ownership of house will be possible by the wealth and cooperation of your husband.

There is a rectangle near the line of Mars at the 37th year of age. There was possibility of house building or of purchasing land at that time. But it seems that the house could not be constructed then, only land was possibly purchased. In my opinion, this land was purchased by you or by someone else in your name.”

“The line of Mars in your hand is very strong and it goes parallel to the life line. Because of the developed mount of Mars, you must have the feeling of somewhat great anger. It appears that you are more than enough watchful about your personality. As and when your ego or self-respect gets hurt, then you become very angry. At that time, you begin washing dirty linens of your antagonist and it is your tendency to make your point of view firm and strong. To make it so, you indulge in all sorts of activities and logic which favour you. Along with it, as already told by me that the line of Mars goes parallel to the life line, it puts self-confidence in you. You must have more than enough self-confidence. You do not get nervous by any sort of obstacle instead. You console your aquaintees during their days of calamity and help them in swimming over their seas of troubles. This is your bright side.

From the mount of Mars, one line has gone to the mount of Saturn and it appears that though it has not touched the fate line, it has certainly reached near it. So it can be assumed that you will not get any benefit from land deals in future.”

“It is my humble advice that you should not go for any land deals in your name. It will be appropriate and favourable for you.

All the other minor lines in your hand are generally favourable. The life line, head line and health line combine to form a triangle. Sometimes the line of Moon also provides this triangle a shape. This triangle is favourable in itself. This triangle clearly indicates that the later half of your life is more favourable and full of happiness. Broadly speaking, if we assume your whole life span as of 68 years, then your favourable period started after the 34th year of your life. This favour and hapiness will continue in your future life. In my opinion, you should worship Lord Vishnu or Lord Krishna to get favour in your life. Besides, the worship of Lord Ram, Goddess Laxmi and other gods and goddesses will help you to get favour.”

The lady raised her head and said, “Panditji, whatever you have said till now is all true but I want to know the answers of some of my questions.”

Panditji replied, “Please ask me without hesitation. I do not have any objection to it.”

The lady smiled and said, “You can know the month of birth by reading one's palm and you can also make out the exact day, date and time of birth through it. I am still in doubt about it. My mother did not remember the exact time of my birth. Sometimes, she tells one time, another time another. There is difference of two to three hours in both the timings.

Panditji replied, “It is not difficult to correct the time of birth through Palmistry. Please wait a minute, I will resolve your problem just now.”

Panditji took out the magnifying glass and studied the lines of palm. He said, “You must be born in April because on the second phalange of the finger of Saturn, there is only one complete line, rest all the lines are broken and faint. If we calculate the date, then by counting the lines, it comes to 8th day of April.”

Waiting for a few moments, Panditji said, “Certainly you were born on April 8th, 1933. So far as the date of birth is concerned, it is verified through reading the line of the palm, you remember the exact date of your birth.”

“Yes Panditji, you have correctly told me the date of my birth through studying the lines of palm. My date of birth is really April 8th, 1933.”

Fixing his eyes on the palm, Panditji further said, “So far as the study of first phalange of your thumb is concerned, it clearly indicates that your sign of zodiac should be cancer and this is just more than two degrees. Thus, your birth time should be about 24 seconds at thirty-one minutes past eight in the evening. This time when calculated, tells that the zodiac of Cancer has past only two degrees.

Your forecast seems to be true because my mother has told me the time between 7 P.M. and 10 P.M. The astrologer who prepared my horoscope has done it with the sign of zodiac as Cancer but he has calculated it as past eight degrees. It seems that he did not know the exact time of my birth. Whatever past events you have told me are fully correct and on that basis, in my opinion, the time told by you is the correct one and the most authentic one.”

Panditji said slowly, “Please consider it fully authentic. Whatever time I have told is true because if one prepares your horoscope on its basis and ascertains the signs of zodiac, then it corroborates with the lines of your palm. Thus, the incidents which became clear by reading the lines of your palm can also be indicated by reading your horoscope.” Waiting for a few moments, Panditji further said, “In a moment, I may also give you a clear picture of the position of different planets when you were born. The sign of zodiac (Lagna) in your horoscope should be Cancer and in the Leo, there should be confluence of your planets with the Leo, the Jupiter, the Moon, the Mars and the Dragon's Tail. In the same way, in the house of Capricorn, there should be Saturn, in Aquarius, there should be Dragon's Head and in Pisces, there should be Sun, Mercury and Venus (all the three to be seated together). In fact, this is the real composition of your horoscope.”

The woman brought out her horoscope from her bag and on perusal found that the same was the position of planets in it, i.e., the one told by Panditji was true.

Concluding the study of her hand, Panditji told her the following more facts :

You do not have any such line in your hand by virtue of which you may get sudden wealth. Neither have you got sudden wealth till now nor will you get it in your future.

You have got the comfort of a vehicle in the beginning of your 44th year of age. It will be a car and it will continue in your future life.

Economically, you do not have any problem or obstacles in your future life.

You have conchshell marks on your little finger and a mark of mountain on your index finger is there. So the last half of your life is favourable and full of happiness.

You have clearly the Laxmi Yog, Brahm Yog, Sadhu Yog, Maha Laxmi Yog, Kalakar Yog, Pativrata Yog, Kailash Yog Kamal Yog and I have described all these combinations in detail in this very book entitled Practical Palmistry. You can look for facts regarding these in the book.

The whole of your future life will be favourable and full of happiness from spiritual point of view. You will die due to heart attack in your house amongst the members of your family and you will die before the death of your husband which is an ideal for an Indian woman.

The woman was fully impressed by the knowledge of Panditji. She got up and touched his feet and said, “The analysis made by you after reading my hand is completely true and fully authentic. Your knowledge in Palmistry is surprising. I have reverence for you and your deep knowledge.”

Panditji replied, “I am not very important in it. We should show reverence and be modest to our ancient literature, our knowledge of Palmistry and our ancestor monks who gave us this knowledge. I am just a means. I only light the lamp of knowledge given by them so that in its light more and more people can find their way; thereby they may make their lives favourable and orderly and may get fullness from all aspects of life.”

“Keeping in view these aims, I have established the Bhartiya Jyotish Anusandhan Kendra at High Court Colony, Jodhpur (Rajasthan) and I have engaged the best pandits here so that people living in India and abroad may get guidance through the study of horoscopes and the study of lines of the palms for their future benefits.”

“I am glad to note that the centre has earned reputation and fame in a very short time. Today, here we get thousands of palmprints and horoscopes for study from India and abroad. All are satisfied here and all have praised the work here.”

“I believe this centre will definitely be able to represent India in the field of astrology.''

Conclusion

Every human being or creature has a part of God and hence, we are called the sons of God. God has all done it orderly and with understanding. If you have been created, then there is mystery in its background or there may be a great necessity. In fact, your creation is only your creation in the whole of the universe. There is none like you in the whole world. It is possible that some are better than you and others are worse than you but the proportion of ingredients in ‘you' is solitary. So it is said that the lines in your hand are your own lines. No two persons in the world can have similar lines. Therefore, all individuals have individuality of personality. God has spread the whole world before you. Now it depends upon you what sort of place should you have and how to create such a place for yourself in this world. For this, it is to be seen how you utilize the moments of your life. Every moment is a moment of gift bestowed on you by God. The moment passed can't come back, though we might spent crores to get it back.

When one moment is more valuable than lakhs and crores of rupees, then you have no authority to spend it carelessly or in useless discussions or in useless activities. You should think that every moment of your life has utility and you have to utilize every moment accurately. It depends upon you that how you cope up with those moments yourself and the world around you. This world is constantly moving and if you also move along with it, you will definitely develop but if your speed is less than the speed of progress of the world, then you will lag behind and the world will go ahead of you. After that, though you do great efforts, you can't cope up with the speed of the moving world.

This whole life is your own. All the moments of life are open before you. You should use even the smallest moment. Recognize every opportunity and close it into your fist. Try to experience the heartbeat of even a moment of the future time and try to make it in your favour. It is in your hand and if you do that, you will see that the lines of your palm are changing and rising above and your steps are moving towards progress, success, honour and fame. What you need is to proceed forward and to accept the garlands which are waiting for you eagerly.

Every work of life has two aspects: the positive and the negative. We can analyse any work in these two aspects or we can say that we are standing between creation and destruction. It depends upon you whether you create the moments of your life or you destroy them. In fact, you are not dead but you are alive, active and moving and keep every moment moving. Your feet should constantly move towards progress.

You should get up, be active and be creative. Be assured that the all-round wide gulf of have nots and frustration will end itself. The thorns of the path will turn into flowers. But it will require self-confidence, courage, a daring attitude and patience. You need a will to move ahead constantly.

You have the time, energy, labour, health and life. Use them with both of your hands. Whatever energy and time you have got, the same was also with Gandhi, Tagore, Tolstoy, Lincoln and Jesus. Then what is the reason that they are still shining like stars in the world and you are wandering in wilderness anonymously. Why are you lagging behind? Why are you tottering? Get up and move ahead, success is before you, victory is calling you. Fame and honour are waiting to welcome you and sucess is standing with a garland to choose you.

Fill your heart and nerve with courage and move ahead. Each of your steps should be firm. Your sight should be able to see the other surface of a thing. It is in your hands to change your weak lines and turn your misfortune into luck. It is time for you to work; your time begins now and it is the call of time of your life.
